The first shoots of oats and wheat are emerging from the freshly tilled fields, and the seedlings of the various vegetables we will be growing this season are already sprouting. The cows are content, protected, and producing delicious creamy milk for the pleasure of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara; the Health and Wellness Center is ready to offer service to the devotees and the surrounding community; and everywhere your presence can be felt through your instructions. You wanted *varṇāśrama* to be a reality, and you wanted your farm communities to be at the vanguard. By following your guidance we see this “Hospital for the Heart” drawing thousands of people a year, who come here for the workshops, retreats, *prasādam*, and festivals, or just to seek shelter from the blazing fire of material existence, as you said they would.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, as the years fall away, we pray that we may more fully comprehend your divine greatness and the scope of your enlightened vision. Please keep us engaged in your service and let us never leave the shelter of your transcendental lotus feet, the shelter of your teachings, and the shelter of your mercy. Allow your legacy to thrive here in the center of Pennsylvania’s heartland, so that “others may be attracted” (*Gītā*-nāgarī evening lecture, 1976) and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.

*Your grateful and loving* disciples, granddisciples, and great-granddisciples at the Gītā-nāgarī farm community in Port Royal, Pennsylvania, USA.  
*(*written by Yaśodāmāyī Devī Dāsī)

## Guyana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Unlike ordinary living entities, who must take birth in this material world, you appeared by your own choice to execute Lord Caitanya’s sacred mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness worldwide. Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura predicted this feat, and the Caitanya-maṅgala foretold your advent, as did the Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa. Your goal was always clear; you never doubted but always had firm faith in the words of your spiritual master. In this way your life was perfectly exemplary.

The pure devotees wander throughout the universe not for their personal, selfish interests but to purify all the conditioned souls. As the 50th anniversary of the founding of ISKCON nears, we in Guyana are making a humble effort to spread your glories to every corner of the country. Our dedicated devotees at the Nimai Pandit Study Centre, near the University of Guyana, are working toward building a brand-new temple there by 2016 as an offering to you. Many innovative initiatives are also planned. And out of your divine mercy you are sending so many very capable devotees who are so diligently assisting in your divine mission here in Guyana. Our humble realization is that whatever good there is in us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is simply the fruit of the endeavor you made. We have had other accomplishments this past year, but we are always aware that whatever we’ve done so far is insufficient.

Please allow us in the Guyana *yātrā* to be an instrument in spreading your glories far and wide. Our foremost duty is to help everyone get an opportunity to receive your blessings, which you so mercifully bestow if we learn the secret of simply linking with your ISKCON. We can only marvel at how much more we have to discover about your transcendental personality. The more you allow us to participate in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, the more we experience newer chapters in this great spiritual adventure. We pray that we can always remember the great contributions you have made to human society, and also the great struggle you underwent to bring the teachings of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to the Western countries.

Our realizations are tiny, and our minds are covered by *anarthas*. Therefore we feel so far away from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Still, we desire to stay under the cool shade of your lotus feet and serve you. Please allow us to be useful; allow us to always stay in the association of your devotees despite having so many faults. You said that all the material opulences in this world are like so many zeroes—absolutely useless. However, if a person adds the “one” of Kṛṣṇa in front of all those zeroes, he will become very rich. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the one who provides that “one.” We pray that we may become a fit candidate to receive that “one” from you, and that we may also help others receive that “one” from you. We pray that we may be engaged in your service always, and we thank you for allowing us the opportunity to be engaged thus far in our lives.

You allowed us to understand the reality of the spiritual world, you allowed us to taste the nectar of chanting the holy names, you gave us service that can qualify us for returning home, back to Godhead, you gave us *mahā*-*prasādam,* which is so dear to all your devotees, and you gave us a reason for wanting to continue living in this world, which is quickly progressing toward hell. How, therefore, can we think for a moment that anything is more important than your instructions and teachings? As a result, out of gratitude the least we can do is simply focus on your desires and make them our life and soul. We should never doubt your words and try to rationalize our inability to fully surrender to you. We should know without any doubt that there can be no happiness in this world without full surrender to Kṛṣṇa and His pure representative.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that our gratitude for what you have done for us will continue to be the motivating force that inspires us to help you in your mission and to struggle to conquer over all obstacles. By forgiveness and tolerance and continuing faith, we can emerge victorious over the tricks of Kali. It is a mistake to limit our conception of you to your manifest pastimes when you were physically present on this earth. We are aware that you are in everything in ISKCON. Please bless us that we can become exclusively and selflessly attached to your glorious service.

We beg your forgiveness for making such a poor offering, and for our boldness in expressing our determination to continue being yours through thick and thin. Still we pray that you will bless us in this coming year with the energy and commitment to better satisfy you by our service. Be compassionate upon us and remove from our hearts all inauspicious things. We are weak and cripple-minded. We depend on your mercy. Kindly keep us in some corner of your fragrant lotus feet.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Guyana.  
(written by Paramātmā Dāsa)

## Hong Kong

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this auspicious day.

You strengthen the devotees in the far-east countries. We are so thankful for your influence in ISKCON’s Hong Kong temple and in China. There are myriads of wandering conditioned souls in China, who, by your grace, can understand the goal of life. By studying your commentated translations of the scriptures and by following the example you have set for us, we have the opportunity to leave this temporary, miserable material world and return to the eternal, blissful abode of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Through your books and lectures, Your Divine Grace guides the devotees with the sublime teachings of the scriptures, which display the scientific knowledge of the creation of the material world and extol the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, the beauty of the spiritual world, and the glories of devotional service. Through the process of devotional service we are gradually becoming purified of material desires and spiritually enlivened. You are so merciful for allowing such fallen, conditioned souls as ourselves to have the shelter of your lotus feet.

*Śrī* Māyāpur *dhāma* is the headquarters of ISKCON. By your blessings the magnificent Temple of the Vedic Planetarium there will help spread the glories of the Lord all over the world. With unflinching determination your disciples and granddisciples have been fulfilling your desire to build the TOVP project. In a few years it will be completed, by you mercy.

We also hope that the project in Śrī Māyāpur will help bring the devotees in Hong Kong, Taiwan, and China into sweet and blissful association with one another and with other devotees from around the globe. It is our sincere desire that people all over the world be blessed and inspired and find their love for God and for one another increasing by the influence of Śrī Māyāpur *dhāma*. May the devotees become empowered to taste the spiritual nectar from the ocean of love brought forth by Lord Caitanya.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your love, your compassion, and your instructions. You make this world a world of rainbows. You and your disciples have been spreading the mercy of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Lord Caitanya, and Lord *Nityānanda* all over the world. Please help us distribute this truth and enlighten people with the knowledge that all work except devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa is bondage. May we remember your love and your instructions in our daily service. May we embrace and cherish all that you have given us. We offer our humble obeisances unto the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī* *jaya*!

Your servants at ISKCON Hong Kong.  
(written by Lalitā Sundarī Devī Dāsī)

## Jagannātha Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our *śata-sahasra-koṭī-daṇḍavat* *praṇāmas*. All glories to you!

*Śrīla Prabhupāda,* at birth your parents may have noticed fearlessness in you, and that is why they named you Abhay. In this way glorification of you started.

Now your disciples and granddisciples glorify you every year on your Appearance Day for what you did for human society. I am very eager to know how your friends and associates glorify you on your present planet. I’m sure all of them must be lagging behind you in rendering service to the Supreme Lord. Do they glorify you for the service you did on this planet, for you service you’re doing there?

Please bestow your mercy on us so that we can also glorify the service you render to the Supreme Enjoyer.

Begging to remain your menial servants,  
Your devotees at ISKCON Purī and Silchar, India.  
(written by Gaura-Nitāi Dāsa)

## Karuna Bhavan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on this day of your auspicious appearance! It is our great fortune and privilege that we are able to take part in this worldwide celebration.

Your life was an unparalleled example of patience, determination, and enthusiasm in all circumstances. Nothing could break your faithfulness to the instructions and desires of your beloved spiritual master, of the *guru*-*paramparā,* and of the cherished Lord of your heart, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You knew success was in His hands. Without having that living ideal before us, how could we expect the possibility of success beyond our dreams to the dream that is yours?

In New Vrindavan in June of 1976, you said “I have given you some framework. Now you fill up. Make it a nice building.” In Los Angeles in May of 1972, you called it a “framework of expansion.” To make a nice building, professional craftsmen are needed, craftsmen who take pride in doing a perfect job with a finishing touch. Similarly, we see how so many thoughtful devotees, grateful for the privilege of serving you, have been innovative and have become expert in their field of preaching. So many nice programs, projects, and departments have been constructed. They give devotees so many tools and resources to initiate, preserve, and deepen their Kṛṣṇa consciousness and that of others. In this way the devotees channel the spiritual energy that flows from your heart, via their hearts, to the hearts of others—an energy that is driven by compassion and the desire to please guru and Kṛṣṇa and deliver all fallen souls. Thus empowered, the members of your spiritual family are increasing by the thousands.

As the servants of your servants, we aspire to follow in their footprints.

Karuṇā Bhavān became famous for its book distribution and for thereby helping other temples financially. “Gaurāṅga” became a household name in Scotland. But so many difficulties also arose that caused some setbacks. This is now history. Those who remain are a dedicated few. As we have matured, we have made mistakes. But we’re not allowing ourselves to be crippled by them or by any accompanying criticism. Senior and more experienced Vaiṣṇavas are assisting us toward great achievements.

We have been making a mark in the yoga scene with *kīrtana* and *prasādam* distribution. In Scotland there’s no competition. The field is practically untouched. When we ask for feedback, the most common response is “Nothing can be improved.”

We toured northern Scotland last year, planting some seeds of interest. We made a few contacts that look promising for big public festivals in the future, but in general a follow-up in the north would be difficult due to distance. It was a lot of hard work and sacrifice for too little a result. So this year we’re planning to focus locally, where many seeds have already been planted by many years of book distribution, upon which Karuṇā Bhavān was built. Continued contact would then be easier, with a view to making more devotees.

So many temples are flourishing by communicating with and taking a personal interest in the members of their congregation. We have been taking help from devotees who have become professional in this field. So far the biggest result is in Edinburgh, where the devotees have maintained a Nāma-haṭṭa continuously for eighteen years. With the introduction of an accompanying Bhakti Vriksha program, a core of very sincere devotees has developed into a small, close-knit community. They meet regularly for *kīrtana* and *Kṛṣṇa* book reading, and they attend the temple at least once a month. For your pleasure, the Edinburgh devotees desire to reestablish the Centre of Vedic Culture there.

Begging for your blessings as we attempt to continue the expansion of your framework in Scotland, we remain  
The servants of your servants at Karuṇā Bhavān, ISKCON Scotland.

## Khaśadesh

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

Your granddisciples in Khaśadesh are showing increasing dedication and progress on the path of *bhakti,* and in sharing it with others. It is by your mercy only that the knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the glories of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu are changing the hearts of so many in this godforsaken land. Just as Lord *Rāma* appreciated the efforts of the spider in its attempt to push small grains of sand to help build the bridge to victory and *dharma,* you kindly encourage us every day in our infantile steps in building a bright spiritual future for many Chinese souls.

The translation of your books is ongoing. Your dedicated team of translators have this year completed the Twelve Cantos of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and other titles are being translated for mass distribution. Each book, each chapter, each word, is a rain of flowers in the hearts of those who, as in a desert, hanker for knowledge of the soul and everlasting happiness away from the heat of the ultramaterialism and impersonalism that plague this vast land. Increasingly, too, your Chinese servants enjoy spending time in the holy *dhāmas* of Śrī Māyāpur and Śrī Vṛndāvana, where they chant and dance with no restrictions for weeks on end, as you instructed. This is also your immense mercy, and we are grateful to you for offering us this opportunity.

The strong family that you created and that you originally placed under the care of your dedicated servant Tamāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami is expanding far and wide. The satellite *yātrās* of Taiwan, Hong Kong, and the Philippines are all harmoniously joined with Khaśadesh, and with the constant presence of your dedicated disciples, who give their love, care, and association in so many ways, we are securely on the path to spiritual success, all the way to your lotus feet.

On this auspicious day we pray that you will shower even more of your kindness, blessings, and mercy upon us for another year of long hours of service, tireless preaching, intense *sādhana,* and the continuous attempt to cleanse our hearts by the chanting of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa. Let us be like soft clay in your perfect hands so we may serve your mission with great devotion and bring pride to the *Gauḍīya* Vaiṣṇava *paramparā*.

Your servants in Khaśadesh (China).

## Kaunas

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

In my teenage years I used to visit the Hare Kṛṣṇa temple with my friends. At that time it was only one of a hundred spots we would visit. Then I ran away from home and started to think, “What really has value for me?” I was surprised to realize that the temple was the place my most dear to my heart. I didn’t know anything about devotional service at the time, but devotees made a deep impression on my soul.

As time passed, your ISKCON movement became the oasis that saved my life. It became much more than a lighthouse in the dark, much more than an antidote for a man dying of poison. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you took the shining *cintāmaṇi* dust of Vṛndāvana and distributed it all around the globe, like Prometheus did with fire. You gave us a place to stay and engagement in Lord Kṛṣṇa’s service. I pray that all people may one day experience cooling effect of the shade of your lotus feet.

Your eternal servants in Lithuania.  
*(written by Saṅkīrtana* Dāsa)

## Kiev

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration on the 118th anniversary of your appearance.

Taking the opportunity of this glorious event, we are trying as far as we can to glorify the limitless ocean of your transcendental qualities, just for our own purification.

You taught us not only by your words but by your own example, just as Lord Caitanya accepted the position of a devotee and taught devotional service by practicing it Himself *(Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* *Ādi* 3.20–21):

*āpani karimu bhakta-bhāva aṅgīkāre*  
*āpani ācari’ bhakti śikhāimu sabāre*

**\[Lord Caitanya said:\] “I shall accept the role of a devotee, and I shall teach devotional service by practicing it Myself.**

*āpane nā kaile dharma śikhāna nā yāya*  
*ei ta’ siddhānta gītā-bhāgavate gāya*

**“Unless one practices devotional service himself, he cannot teach it to others. This conclusion is indeed confirmed throughout the** ***Gītā*** **and** ***Bhāgavatam*****.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said that your books are nondifferent from you, and this is the absolute truth.

The ninth chapter of the *Ādi*-*līlā* of *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta* describes how Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself became the tree of devotional service and its gardener:

I take shelter of the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu, who Himself is the tree of transcendental love of Kṛṣṇa, its gardener, and also the bestower and enjoyer of its fruits. Thus the Lord brought the desire tree of devotional service to this earth and became its gardener. He sowed the seed and sprinkled upon it the water of His will. By His inconceivable powers, the Lord became the gardener, the trunk, and the branches simultaneously. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* *Ādi* 9.6, 9, 12\]

Your ISKCON is also a branch of the Caitanya tree of devotional service, and you, like Lord Caitanya, are also its gardener, for you are carefully nourishing this growing tree until one day it will include all the countries, cities, villages, and houses in this world. And, like Lord Caitanya, you are distributing the fruits of the tree by the handful to all the poor and hungry people:

The transcendental gardener, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu distributed handful after handful of fruit in all directions, and when the poor, hungry people ate the fruit, the gardener smiled with great pleasure. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* *Ādi* 9.30)

We witnessed all of this during last year’s Bhakti Sangama festival, when thousands of devotees and guests participated in wonderful and powerful *kīrtana,* and all their faces reflected your divine smile. In this way your life will be an example for ten thousand years, bringing the highest benefit to others.

You are the example of how to carry out this famous order of Lord Caitanya’s:

*bhārata-bhūmite haila manuṣya janma yāra*  
*janma sārthaka kari’ kara para-upakāra*

**One who has taken his birth as a human being in the land of India \[Bhārata-varṣa\] should make his life successful and work for the benefit of all other people. \[*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta,*** ***Ādi*** **9.41\]**

You are the perfect example of how to do this with compassion and the tolerance of a tree. Anyone who personally came in contact with you attained the highest eternal benefit. We can see this by contemplating the lives of your disciples who for the last forty years or so have unceasingly and tirelessly helped you distribute the fruits of Lord Caitanya’s tree.

Please empower us to deliver your sublime message to the world purely, without any distortion, by cooperating in your service.

Your servants at the New Navadvīpa Mandir in Kiev, Ukraine.  
(written by Bhairavatarjaka Dāsa)

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances. All glories to you and this amazing ISKCON movement spreading like wildfire across the globe!

Another year has passed, and sometimes it is hard to keep up with all that is happening in our small corner of the world.

Over the last year we have seen here in London some amazing progress in the areas of community development, team building, and focus on a new temple for Their Most Beautiful Lordships *Śrī Śrī* Rādhā-Londonīśvara.

Last year we initiated a project to focus on trying to fulfill your vision for London, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We know how important this was to you. It is our strong desire that your most beloved Deities Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara be situated in a facility that is befitting. We truly want to please you and the whole *paramparā*.

We started a petition to acquire land in Regent’s Park and are working on designs for something special—something more than just a temple, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We want to erect a holy place of transcendental pastimes that will attract unlimited souls to the service of the Lord and bring together devotees in order to deepen their loving exchanges and their faith in Guru and Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are in need of your mercy and guidance. Please help us become pure instruments of your divine will and desire. Please help us co-operate to satisfy you and Their Lordships.

We simply want to become your humble and surrendered servants.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at ISKCON London (Soho), UK.

## London (South)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious day!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, instead of staying in Vṛndāvana, you spent your valuable time giving us the sweet nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Even if all of us thank you from the bottom of our hearts for this effort for the rest of our lives, it will not be enough. Your books are saving the whole world and are also the safety net protecting us from the material miseries we encounter daily. We do not know where we would be without your divine grace and your transcendental books.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are indeed the *jagad-guru* of all the lost souls. All your granddisciples are united only by you. Even though we may all have different *dīkṣā*-*gurus,* you are the *śikṣā*-*guru* of all living entities. At ISKCON South London, devotees have come together due to your books and teachings. We have been able to do some small amount preaching due to the powerful weapons you provided us for this age—your books. Lord Caitanya gave us the *yuga-dharma,* the chanting of the holy names, and you gave us the *yuga* weapons, your books.

During last year’s Christmas marathon the whole temple was so inspired to distribute your books that we were able to go out every day and far surpassed all our previous efforts. We have tried to place your books in many of the houses in Croydon and hope to place *Bhagavad-gītās* in all the schools in the area.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for spending your precious time translating and writing so many books. These books are invaluable in our preaching. Your blessings are also with each and every devotee who reads any of your books.

*nṛ-deham ādyaṁ su-labhaṁ su-durlabhaṁ*  
*plavaṁ su-kalpaṁ guru-karṇadhāram*  
*mayānukūlena nabhasvateritaṁ*  
*pumān bhavābdhiṁ na taret sa ātma-hā*

**“The human body, which can award all benefit in life, is automatically obtained by the laws of nature, although it is a very rare achievement. This human body can be compared to a perfectly constructed boat having the spiritual master as the captain and the instructions of the Personality of Godhead as favorable winds impelling it on its course. Considering all these advantages, a human being who does not utilize his human life to cross the ocean of material existence must be considered the killer of his own soul.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.20.17)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, this year, by your grace, the devotees put in more effort in many ways:

• We held two Diwali festivals outside the temple, which attracted many new people to Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

• We visited sixteen schools, where we were allowed to do Diwali presentations to a total of approximately four thousand children. The children were very receptive to the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*.

• During the month of Kārttika, we held *sat-saṅgas* in a different home every day, and more than a thousand devotees offered candles to Lord Dāmodara during the month.

• As mentioned above, the Christmas marathon was our most successful yet. Many devotees participated in the distribution of your *Bhagavad-gītā,* and, thanks to your divine grace, they were enthused by this service.

• Recently we held a very successful Rāma Navamī festival at the Oshwal Hall. This festival attracted five hundred people, who chanted the holy names and enjoyed performances of Bharat Natyam dances and a drama performed by the Bhaktivedanta Players.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that you will continue to keep us engaged in your service.

Your foolish disciples at ISKCON South London, UK.

## Malaga

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*titikṣavaḥ kāruṇikāḥ suhṛdaḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*ajāta-śatravaḥ śāntāḥ sādhavaḥ sādhu-bhūṣaṇāḥ*

***“*****The symptoms of a sādhu are that he is tolerant, merciful, and friendly to all living entities. He has no enemies. He is peaceful, he abides by the scriptures, and all his characteristics are sublime. (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **3.25.21)**

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains.” (Śrīdhara Svāmī’s commentary on** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.1*****)***

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā!

In 1965, at the age of 69, you left the shores of India and embarked on your journey to the Western world to spread the holy names of Lord Kṛṣṇa and deliver the fallen souls from the nescience of material existence. In spite of your advanced age and your enduring all kinds of difficulties, you were determined to fulfill the desire of your spiritual master, Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, to propagate the science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western world. You had complete confidence in the ultimate success of your mission because of your firm faith in the prophecy of Caitanya Mahāprabhu that His holy name would be chanted in every town and village in the world. Because of your full surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa and your taking shelter of the lotus feet of your spiritual master, you were able to accomplish your mission.

For you, preaching was always the essence. This you stated in your own Vyāsa-pūjā offering to your Guru Mahārāja in 1961, which you called *Vaiśiṣṭyāṣṭakam*. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the savior of the fallen souls in this material world. Without your kindness, we would never have known our real goal of life as human beings, nor would we ever have known that the Supreme Personality of Godhead is Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Some verses from your *Vaiśiṣṭyāṣṭakam:*

12\. To see a sense enjoyer is just like drinking poison, but you, O my master, go far and near, even abroad, to give them your *darśana* \[and the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu\].

13\. *Mlecchas* and *yavanas* are forbidden to enter the Hindu temples, but you, my lord, made them the chairmen and sat them in the assembly of devotees.

15\. In the cities of Kali-yuga the instructions of the bona fide spiritual preceptors are “forbidden”; still you remain there in any way possible.

16\. The devotees want to hide in a secluded place to perform their *bhajana*. You, however, do not accept this in your judgment.

17\. Wherever there is an increase in population, in that place preaching work is to be found.

Below are some other verses from Your Divine Grace’s *Vaiśiṣṭyāṣṭakam*, written as you were preparing your First Canto volumes of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and planning your worldwide mission. *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, you always had a very clear Kṛṣṇa conscious vision; you were *dṛḍha-vrata,* absolutely resolute in your determination to deliver the world.

25\. But simply a festival of flowers and fruits does not constitute worship. The one who serves the message of the guru really worships him.

26\. The service of the message is the real meaning of the *Vedas*. Don’t be proud, brothers. Come back to this.

27\. Kalidas Nag \[a learned man who was defeated by Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura and later became his disciple\] said in a public forum one day:

28\. “Kali’s mission is to kill the entire world while the essential meaning of Lord Caitanya’s message is kept locked in a cage.”

In the *Caitanya-candrodaya-nāṭakam* it is said that Kali (the personification of the age of quarrel and hypocrisy) became sad upon the appearance of Lord Caitanya. Up until then, Kali’s main soldiers—irreligion, falsehood, lust, anger, and greed—were very proud that in this age they had already captured and disabled all the saintly persons by their powerful influence. But seeing their master, Kali, very sad, these soldiers asked him, “Why are you so afraid of just one inexperienced son of a *brāhmaṇa*? We have already defeated all the great Vedic scholars and learned *paṇḍitas*. What can this one *brāhmaṇa* boy do?”

Kali replied, “Oh, you do not know that this *brāhmaṇa* boy is not ordinary. I foresee that He will purify all the fallen souls. Simply by his *saṅkīrtana* he will turn Kali-yuga into Satya-yuga. He is Lord Nārāyaṇa Himself. Indeed, I’m fortunate that He is appearing in my jurisdiction. Even though I will be defeated, I will also be delivered.”

Only Lord Caitanya and His empowered generals like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can change the face of the earth. The *Caitanya*-*bhāgavata* (*Ādi*-*khaṇḍa* 2.159) says that devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu are so powerful that each of them can deliver the whole universe.

So we gather together to express our gratitude to you. We cannot truly understand the gigantic blessings you have given us through your herculean preaching efforts, your patience, your dedication, and, most of all, your compassion. There is no one in the three worlds who can give anyone something more valuable than what you gave us.

In the present degraded age, when we are threatened from every side, when even the concept of spirituality is often corrupted, what would have been our fate without the blessings you gave us? If we could truly understand the magnitude of your blessings, we would know that there is nothing else for us to do but distribute your books or in some other way help book distribution.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, forgive us for not yet having inundated the world with your books. This is our obligation. So please forgive us, for we still haven’t absorbed ourselves completely in the service of your lotus feet. Therefore please give us your blessings so we can do it and thus fully express our gratitude to you for your mercy upon us.

We ask for your blessings because on our own we will not be able to inundate the world with your books. Only through your mercy can we do this and much more. Thank you a million times and more, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

We wish to achieve your blessings so that we will become expert at serving the lotus feet of your servants. Śrīla Prabhupāda, that’s all we can present to you as our humble offering. We pray for your mercy and the mercy of your disciples so that you and they may continue to guide us in our attempt to progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda. May your glories be proclaimed throughout the three worlds.

Your humble, faithful followers and great admirers at ISKCON Malaga, Spain.

## Manchester

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most sincere humble obeisances. All glories unto you, our dear lord and master!

Please accept our deepest heartfelt gratitude for your kind gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thank you for the International Society of Krishna Consciousness, a transcendental gateway to our eternal home, the abode of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.

Within this transcendental gateway, ISKCON, lie all truths, the acts of devotion, and all blissful symptoms of loving service. ISKCON is your gift of rejuvenation and the oasis of transcendental nectar that we are forever seeking. However, due to our material vision, sometimes this oasis appears to be ever distant and to be imposing a constant uphill struggle for success. Due to foolishness and being blinded by our material conditioning, we cannot understand the real essence of spiritual progress.

You have given us everything we need within the society of ISKCON. There is nothing to add or remove. Everything is there in its full splendor, but that splendor is invisible to the untrained eye, just as gemstones are appreciated only by those who understand their value.

Sometimes visions of failure emerge due to past conditioning and the lack of determined enthusiasm, and these visions lead us to emphasize imperfections rather than seek viable solutions. While it can be easy in these circumstances to become absorbed in the desire for recognition (whether it be knowingly or unknowingly), we unwittingly place ourselves in deeper illusion and frustration. Your Divine Grace has very nicely described the true path of spiritual emancipation: cooperation. Within the spirit of cooperation to please you, we can truly find all answers we seek. The heart and mind increasingly become blissfully surrendered at your lotus feet, where we find an ever-shining shelter of hope, loving service, and deep satisfaction, for which we are eternally grateful. Therefore we pray wholeheartedly that this spirit of cooperation may ever be present in our hearts.

ISKCON Manchester continues its preaching mission, focused on three key areas, namely outreach preaching, *prasadam* distribution, and congregational chanting of the holy names. The core congregation of devotees are *gṛhasthas* with children, and that fact has led us to promote family worship of the Supreme Lord, creating an attractive setting for visitors and the public at large.

Over the past year some notable changes have taken place here at ISKCON Manchester. The number of core devotees has reduced significantly due to devotees’ jobs or family commitments taking them to other places. While this reduction has affected how much we can reach out and support further expansion, it has also given us an opportunity to concentrate on the remaining core devotees and, where possible, re-allocate responsibilities to those who are able. Currently ISKCON Manchester has no full-time serving devotees, and so we are fully dependent on volunteers.

By the Lord’s grace we are seeing more new visitors to ISKCON Manchester from diverse backgrounds. While this is very encouraging, it will take some time before we see the fruits— these individuals’ becoming committed devotees. The other challenge the management faces with the reduced core devotee congregation is the increased demand of temple services on the remaining initiated devotees. We are greatly fortunate that they are so dedicated and willing, but there service time is limited because they have to balance their temple services with responsibilities relating to work, home, and children.

We continue to support outreach preaching via Nāma-haṭṭa groups in Bolton, Stoke-on-Trent, Warrington, North Wales, and Stockport, and we are working with the Scottish *yātrā* to support other areas, such as Preston, Leeds, and Liverpool. We hope that these *yātrās* will gradually develop stronger, more self-sufficient devotees and increase the congregational links during key festivals.

We host more than 250 visitors at key festivals, which we now hold at external venues, helping to widen our outreach to the community at large. His Grace Jagadātmā Prabhu runs the Stockport storefront center full-time with very little resources, and he has done extremely well by attracting local interest. He has recently started Sunday Feast programs to reinforce a stronger local focus on Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Śloka* recitation classes for children are going on in Bolton and Manchester. We hope this foundational training for younger devotees will help train future preachers and *brāhmaṇas* for the mission.

We hold five main annual events to create increased traction for the Northwest ISKCON mission. These are the North Spring Retreat, Manchester Ratha-yātrā, Gaura Pūrṇimā festival, Janmāṣṭamī festival, and twelve-hour *kīrtana* during Kārttika. These events stimulate immense cooperation and significantly cement loving relationships among devotees and visitors alike.

Kindly bless us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that we will always focus on the needs of the devotees who are dedicating their lives to helping push forward your missionary activities. Please guide us to keep Lord Kṛṣṇa in the center of all our activities by deeply understanding your desires to serve Him in all circumstances. May we see challenges as great opportunities for advancement in devotional service, and may we always seek to find solutions that bring greater unity despite apparent diversity.

We beg to truly embrace the many different ways devotional service is rendered. Please increase our maturity so we may deeply transform the core of our hearts and imbibe the virtue of selfless loving service.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Manchester, UK.

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, for you have blessed us all with the privilege of devotional service.

On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, we are especially appreciating your gift of the devotional process of Deity worship. In February 1936, in your Vyāsa-pūjā offering to your spiritual master, His Divine Grace *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī* Prabhupāda, you wrote a poem containing this stanza:

Absolute is sentient  
Thou hast proved,  
Impersonal calamity  
Thou hast removed.

Your spiritual master very much liked your poem, and especially these lines. It showed him that you had fully realized the essence of his teachings, namely, that the Absolute Truth is the Supreme Person and that we each have an eternal personal relationship with Him. Nearly thirty years later, when you journeyed to the United States to preach, you continued to propagate the same essential truth. Understanding that the Western world was heavily covered by passion and ignorance and impersonal tendencies, you took the bold step of introducing the devotional process of Deity worship to Westerners.

The International Society for Krishna Consciousness is popularly known as The Hare Kṛṣṇa Movement because everything is centered on chanting the holy names of the Lord, especially the Hare Krsna *mahā-mantra*. You revealed to us the life of Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura—how he chanted 300,000 holy names of the Lord each day and propagated the importance of chanting the holy names. You also warned us not to imitate him by staying in a secluded place and attempting to chant *japa* all day. You knew that none of us are able to follow such a rigorous practice and that we needed to be engaged in many kinds of attractive devotional activities. Śrīla *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī* has enumerated sixty-four activities by which a novice devotee practicing *vaidhi-sādhana-bhakti* can engage all his senses in the service of the Lord. Among these activities, he has selected five as principal:

1\. Hearing *Śrīmad*-*Bhāgavatam*

2\. Association with advanced devotees

3\. Living in a sacred place, such as Mathurā

4\. Chanting the holy name of the Lord

5\. Serving the Deity form of the Lord with great faith

Of the fifty-nine remaining items, at least thirty-five are directly related to Deity worship. That makes the fifth item, serving the Lord in His Deity form, especially significant, since it includes so many ways to serve the Lord. Deity worship itself can be broken down into sixty-four items, and many of those find their counterparts in the original sixty-four items given by Śrīla *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī*.

One of the practical benefits of Deity worship is that it helps us change our consciousness from being absorbed in material sense gratification to being absorbed in satisfying Lord Kṛṣṇa. Practically speaking, the most miserable thing in life is to work hard for our personal happiness. The harder we try to become happy ourselves, independent of the Lord’s service, the more unhappy we become. In Deity worship we regularly serve Lord Kṛṣṇa by dressing Him, feeding Him, holding festivals for Him, chanting His holy names, revealing our minds to Him, and more. By feeling the satisfaction of beautifully dressing the Lord, we gradually lose the desire to decorate our own bodies. By cooking wonderful preparations for the Lord to eat, we gradually become so satisfied that we lose our greed to eat opulent food. By working cooperatively to hold wonderful festivals for the Lord, we enjoy those festivals so much that we lose our desire for mundane parties and social events.

Lord Kṛṣṇa is always our well-wishing friend, and He is present in our heart to guide us. The Deity of Kṛṣṇa is identical to the Lord in the heart, and He understands all our happiness and distress, anxieties and desires. Just as we can reveal our mind to a friend and get relief from some intense anxiety we are holding within, so we can reveal our mind to the Deity and get that same relief. As a close friend may offer some good advice to help us, so the Deity, from within the heart, will reciprocate with us. For example, we may feel upset about the actions of some devotee and want to confront him or her, but at the same time we know that emotionally we will get hurt, or that we may make an offense by directly dealing with the situation ourselves. If we reveal our feelings to the Deity, He may tell us, “Trust Me, I will take care of the problem better than you can.” If we follow the Lord’s guidance, we will see that He will take care of the situation in the best way possible and that we will not be hurt emotionally or spiritually. We will also increase our faith in Him and love for Him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us many detailed instructions on how to worship the Deities. For example, you have taught us to not keep *prasādam* and *bhoga* in the same refrigerator. Sometimes we do not take those rules so seriously, but you explained that if we are not even willing to follow some simple rules then how can we show Lord Kṛṣṇa we want to love Him? If we follow the rules and guidelines for Deity worship under your direction, that endeavor will show the Lord we want to advance in spiritual life, and He will reciprocate by granting us that advancement.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving the world the privilege of Deity service. You took a great risk by engaging your disciples in Deity worship, since the Deity is the Lord Himself and we are not qualified for such intimate service to the Lord. Here at ISKCON Miami we pray that you will send more devotees who are eager to serve our Deities and that you will guide those who render such service how to do so with a pure heart. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your humble servants at ISKCON Miami, Florida, USA.

## Midrand

Dearest Srila Prabhupada,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Loving Grace! All glories to your *Śrī* Vyāsa-pūjā!

So much trouble to gift us this process you have undergone.  
In every endeavor, you did the Lord depend upon.  
When times got tougher, your devotion by purity and devotion you fortified.  
Steadfastly with every word and deed you the Lord have glorified.  
The lesson of your devoted life is thus most instructive,  
Teaching us how to cross Māyās hurdles and to remain devotionally  
productive.  
You afforded us the best of everything so causelessly,  
Saying that if we felt indebted we should preach this message vigorously.  
Our attempts here in the last two years are possible only by your mercy—  
Many newspaper articles, regular gatherings, and book distribution weekly;  
Food for life monthly; regular hall programs and courses based on your  
teachings;  
New souls becoming inspired, and here in Midrand a new temple opening.  
To please you in service is our only desire and fervent prayer.  
To create a loving family of servitors we do aspire.  
May our humble attempts to Your Divine Grace bring pleasure.  
We depend your words “The grandfather is more merciful than the father.”

Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Midrand, Johannesburg, South Africa.  
*(*written by Mādrī Devī Dāsī)

## Montreal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear *Śrīla Prabhupāda*,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on this auspicious day of your divine appearance.

*Śrī* *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu predicted that in as many towns and villages as there are on the surface of the earth, His holy name will be preached: *pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma/ sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma* (*Caitanya-bhāgavata,* *Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126)

The Lord showered His mercy on you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that you could go to many countries and make devotees of thousands of people who had never before heard of Kṛṣṇa or had any knowledge of the Vedic civilization. *Kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana:* “Without being specially empowered by Kṛṣṇa, no one can preach His glories all over the world.” It is amazing that wherever one goes on this planet there is an ISKCON temple nearby, where devotees follow the standard temple programs, perform *harināma,* distribute your books and *prasādam*, etc..

Another reason for your success is that you had unflinching faith in Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master, His Divine Grace *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. You took to heart the order of your spiritual master to spread Kṛṣṇa’s glories all around the world.

In 1952 you launched the League of Devotees in Jhansi, aiming to widely preach the invaluable knowledge of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* by which one can be delivered from the clutches of *māyā* and go back to the spiritual world.

You started *Back to Godhead* magazine single-handedly and went out daily in the oppressive heat of a New Delhi summer to sell the magazines at tea stalls.

To preach in the West you would have to have books, and so you started translating *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and by Lord Kṛṣṇa’s arrangement and your Guru Mahārāja’s blessing you arrived in New York in 1965 with only forty rupees.

In the early days you met with many obstacles, but nothing could discourage you from your mission. Your tape recorder was stolen when you lived at Dr. Mishra’s office, and later you had to live on the Bowery in a madman’s loft, and you had to walk in areas filled with dirt, bums, hippies, drug addicts, weirdos, and drunks lying on the street.

Your deep love for Kṛṣṇa was manifested by your constant endeavor to establish the mission of *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu. Your purity, deep conviction, knowledge, and love of God attracted young hippies, who for the first time found someone they could trust, and a few sincerely followed your guidance.

After successfully opening centers in New York and San Francisco, you sent devotees to Montreal in 1967. The following year you came here for a few months to bless our city. The devotees, by your mercy, opened a temple on Park Avenue; they would go out every day for *harināma* and book distribution. In the early days the devotees had a rough time with the police, who would sometimes break their neckbeads and then throw the devotees in jail overnight. Yet they were not discouraged, because they were confident that you were showering your unlimited grace on them. They were fully surrendered at your lotus feet. Although they were living in an austere condition, they were very happy to serve in your mission.

In 1975 you came to visit our newly acquired temple on Pie IX boulevard, near the Olympic stadium. After the *guru*-*pūjā* you gave a wonderful class, encouraging us to become more Kṛṣṇa conscious in our daily activities. Later the devotees gave you a tour of the building. Just by the dust of your lotus feet, our temple has become a place of pilgrimage.

Today your devotees here go out regularly on *harināma* and distribute your books and *prasādam* with full enthusiasm. All our major festivals attract many people, and we have regular visits from high school and university students eager to know about our philosophy.

Recently we opened a new preaching center in a predominantly French area; it’s called The Bhakti Center. By your blessings we hope to spread the glories of Vedic knowledge and expand our preaching activities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an ocean of mercy for many reasons, but especially because of your books, which have saved many lives around the world. Your books have been translated into all the major languages of the world and are the law books for the next ten thousand years. Many people have been introduced to your books on the internet. For example, recently we received a phone call from a person who read your books while browsing the internet. He lives quite far from Montreal, but after reading some of your books online he started chanting the *mahā-mantra*, and now he wants to visit our temple and meet the devotees. There are many amazing *saṅkīrtana* stories in Montreal—about how your books have affected the lives of people who have received them. You are saving us all. It is inconceivable how much good you have done for the human society by giving transcendental knowledge through your books.

Your books have given us a clear vision about how to cross over the nescience of this material world. That is why you said we must continue to distribute your books.

*Śrī-guru-caraṇe rati, ei se uttama gati, je prasāde pure sarva āśā.* The topmost achievement in spiritual life is devotion to the lotus feet of the spiritual master. By his mercy all spiritual aspirations are fulfilled.

If we want success in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we have to follow your instructions, just as you followed your spiritual master’s instructions. “One should accept the instruction of a bona fide spiritual master as one’s mission in life.” (*Bhagavad-gītā* 2.41, purport ).

Please bless us to become more humble and meek yet bold in our preaching activities. You told us to be careful how we spend *lakṣmī*. Any donations we receive are actually yours; so please help us remember this simple fact and guard against unnecessary spending.

Please guide us so that we do not change what you have given us and we maintain the institution you have wonderfully left us for the benefit of mankind.

You have always stressed that we work cooperatively and expand the service of the Lord without personal motivation. Please give us the strength to follow this instruction and to always remember not to add anything in the name of preaching that was not practiced by you or by our previous *ācāryas*. We pray that we do not deviate from the guidelines you gave us.

Lord *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu freely distributed pure love for Kṛṣṇa. Because of your exclusive dedication to *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu, you have brought this matchless gift to all mankind. The whole world is indebted to you.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we are humbly begging for your mercy so that despite our disqualifications we will remain strong in devotional service.

Your servants at the ISKCON temple in Montreal, Quebec, Canada.

## Mumbai (Chowpatty)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace and the *guru*-*paramparā*!

A few days ago I was listening to your audio recordings of 1977. That was the year I heard you on the terrace of your apartment at Juhu, where you spoke to a small group of us. You spoke some very profound truths then that have been deeply embedded in my consciousness ever since.

When you were asked about your leaving Vṛndāvana to travel to the West, you stated that you never left Vṛndāvana because you took Vṛndāvana in your heart all over the world. You said that Kṛṣṇa also resided in your heart and helped you write the *Kṛṣṇa* book and all the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* purports. So you were always spiritually with your guru and Kṛṣṇa, though you were apparently alone in New York in 1965–66.

In February 1977, in Māyāpur, when you spoke at the Appearance Day ceremony of His Divine Grace *Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura*, you stated another profound truth. After quoting Kṛṣṇa’s famous verse explaining how he comes to this world whenever there are discrepancies in people’s following real *dharma* (*yadā yadā hi dharmasya glānir bhavati bhārata. tadātmānaṁ sṛjāmy aham*), you stated that Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotees also repeatedly appear somewhere in these vast material universes to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness and preach the Absolute Truth, and when their mission is accomplished they depart. So whereas somewhere you are taking birth on a planet where you will one day preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness as you did here, in another universe devotees are seeing you leave after establishing a strong ISKCON.

We are therefore eternally grateful for all your mind-, intelligence-, and heart- cleansing books, your lectures, *prasādam,* temples, the holy name, senior leaders (your disciples), and the unlimited number of other gifts you bestowed on us and all people of the world. These are truly the “Wonders of the World” now, which can convert the whole world into Vṛndāvana, the land of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In the service of the *guru*-*paramparā* and Lord Kṛṣṇa, we remain

Your insignificant servants in Mumbai (Juhu), India.  
(written by Śrī Nāthjī Dāsa)

## Mumbai (Juhu)

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

We have acquired a farmhouse on a four-and-a-half-acre plot near your Nilachal Farm in Talasari. This land has plenty of water, even in summer, which will help the Nilachal Farm, which dries up in summer. Several rooms in the farmhouse are used for preaching purposes. We beg for your blessings to increase the preaching activities here.

We recently started receiving pure cow’s milk and organic produce from our Talasari farm for your beloved Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī. We are also completing the permanent structure of the *gośālā,* for the comfort of your cows. Please bless us so that we can preach to the world your formula “simple living, high thinking.”

During last December’s book distribution marathon we distributed 220,024 *Bhagavad-gītās,* number two out of all temples in the world. This is the first time we ever distributed more than two lakhs of *Bhagavad-gītās* during the December marathon. We also came in third in the world for our yearly book distribution score, which included 648,957 *mahā*-big books. By your divine mercy may we distribute more books this year.

In February of this year we held Lord Jagannātha’s Ratha-yātrā, starting from the Bhaktivedanta Swami Mission School in Sastri Nagar and culminating in Juhu Vile Parle Scheme. In the pandal program that followed the Ratha-yātrā, devotees from Poland and other parts of the world, led by His Holiness Indradyumna Swami, presented the KrishnaFest cultural program. All participants were inspired by the various items presented. By your divine grace may these preaching activities be expanded in the future.

During our Śrī Rāma Navamī festival this year, we held Śrī Śrī *Sītā*-*Rāma*’s *abhiṣeka* for *kalash* donors in the evening, which most donors appreciated. During the festival, His Grace Sārvabhauma Prabhu recited Śrī Rāma Kathā for five days, a program very well received by many members of our enthusiastic congregation. Please bless us to expand this celebration in the future.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know you are displeased by unnecessary demolition of constructed buildings so we can make something new. Therefore, since your school in Hare Krishna Land was shifted to its new premises in Shastri Nagar, we have been renovating the former school building with minimum demolition. The renovated building will house an ashram, a dormitory, guest rooms, and offices. Kindly give us the intelligence to renovate the building nicely for your transcendental pleasure.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for engaging us in the service of your dear Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rāsabihārījī.

Your insignificant servants at ISKCON Mumbai (Juhu), India.

## Nagpur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We wish you the best of Vyāsa-pūjā celebrations on this most auspicious day.

ISKCON Nagpur has grown over the last year in many ways, and on Gaura Pūrṇimā we inaugurated the new temple in the center of the city.

We feel there is great potential for spreading Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission here, and we pray for your mercy so we will have the purity and strength to walk in your footsteps and selflessly serve your mission.

We all feel that the time to “boil the milk” has come, since we now have many devotees and further growth will depend on purity. We have very much taken to heart the instructions you gave Haṁsadūta Prabhu in 1972 to “boil the milk” to insure the spiritual strength of the devotees, and we intend to do that in Nagpur.

Please accept this poem-prayer:

The Age of Kali has descended on us like a black cloud in the sky,  
Throwing us into the clutches of *Māyā* like an insignificant fluttering fly.  
For a long time our consciousness lay in deep slumber  
As we, lo!, ran around in this rat-race blunder.  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda! We pray for your causeless mercy  
As we strive to attain our transcendental positions in urgency.  
Give us the power to become more civilized  
And follow the four principles without being demonized.  
Our material body is a golden cage we perceive—  
Please give us the divine intelligence to conceive  
That the self is not this body we receive  
But the soul, whose freedom we must achieve.  
O divine father! Show us the righteous path to divinity,  
So that we may practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness with sensitivity.  
Let us follow ISKCON’s path of brotherhood, we pray to thee,  
So that our society can grow into eternity!!

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Hare Kṛṣṇa!

Your devotees at ISKCON Nagpur, India.

## New Badarikāśrama

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you!

You have given us so many spiritual shelters: your temples, your devotees, your books, all the different services—*sādhana, kīrtana, harināma,* Deity worship, outreach—and what to speak of *prasādam*! We are eternally indebted to you for all these transcendental gifts. You have said that the disciple can never repay the debt owed to the spiritual master but that he can try by giving to others what the spiritual master has given him.

By your divine grace, this month seven devotees will be receiving *dīkṣā* from your disciples His Holiness Devāmrita Swami and His Grace Vaiśeṣika Prabhu. Since last year the devotees here have developed a *saṅkīrtana* training program that is building momentum and generating great enthusiasm for all the temple programs. Govinda's restaurant has re-opened and is distributing the mercy. There are a handful of senior devotees whose steadiness in service is a shining example for all of us. We pray to you on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā that we may be instruments in your hands in serving Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda.

You once said that we will show our love for you by how we cooperate to push on this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in your absence. Here at New Badarikāśrama we pray to be empowered by you to work together so that we can please you and Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda.

Your humble servants at New Badarikāśrama, Denver, Colorado, USA.

## New Biharvan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious occasion of the anniversary of your divine appearance on this planet, please accept our most humble obeisances at your most merciful lotus feet. All glories to you and to your auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā celebrations, which are going on in every part of the world.

How fortunate we are to have come in touch with you in a remote place like Boise, Idaho! In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya* 19.151) Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu tells *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī*:

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**“All living entities are wandering throughout the entire universe. Some of them are being elevated to the upper planetary systems, and some are going down into the lower planetary systems. Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of the creeper of devotional service.”**

We feel very grateful for the matchless gift of pure love of God that you have offered us. We pray that we may not take this gift cheaply, and that we can gradually come to appreciate just how exalted a gift this is. In a letter to one of your disciples, you give us a glimpse of how great you are and how immense this gift is:

It is a basic principle that one must accept a bona fide spiritual master in order to achieve the highest perfection of life, love of God. I thank all of you very much for accepting me as your spiritual master, and I promise that I will take you back to home, back to Godhead. I ask you all to promise me to always chant at least sixteen rounds, follow the regulative principles, read our books and try to preach this Krsna Consciousness Movement all over the world. So far my qualifications are concerned, I am simply trying to carry out the order of my Guru Maharaja. \[Letter to *Nityānanda*, 12 November 1971\]

The gift you are giving us is the ultimate gift of eternal devotional service, as described in the song you gave us to sing every morning during Tulasī *ārati*:

My desire is that you will also give me a residence in the pleasure groves of Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma. Thus within my vision I will always behold the beautiful pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. I beg you to make me a follower of the cowherd damsels of Vraja. Please give me the privilege of devotional service and make me your own maidservant. This very fallen and lowly servant of Kṛṣṇa prays, “May I always swim in the love of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Govinda.

You are an eternal resident of the spiritual world. You are one of the confidential servitors of Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī*. You are a pure devotee of the Lord, and therefore you are fully empowered to take us back to Godhead. We are fallen and ignorant, having given up the service of Kṛṣṇa. On this day of your appearance, we humbly pray to you that we may always remember your mercy and never disrespect you or your kind-hearted devotees.

We beg that together we may be able to do something to please you and your devotees, and help spread your *saṅkīrtana* mission. We humbly beg you to kindly pick us up from this ocean of birth and death and place us as one of the atoms at your lotus feet.

The past year was a good one at your Boise temple. We had some very wonderful devotees join your *saṅkīrtana* party here in Boise. Due to the concerted, dedicated efforts of many devotees residing in Boise, we had many wonderful festivals throughout the year, and these festivals were well attended. By your mercy the devotee community here is also growing and maturing, and we now have a steady congregation during the Friday and Sunday programs. The atmosphere in the temple is pleasant, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Baṅkebihārī look ever more beautiful and smile favorably upon Their devotees, and that is our only success. At the same time, there is so much more we can do to spread your divine movement and so many ways in which we could improve. We are in great need of your mercy and guidance.

Your aspiring servants at New Biharvān Dhāma, ISKCON, Boise, Idaho, USA.  
(written by Gopāl Hari Dāsa)

## New Caitanya Candrodaya Mandira

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept us as your followers. Everything we know and everything we have have come from you.

The order of your spiritual master was ever your guiding light as you set out to spread Lord Caitanya’s mission in foreign lands. So firm was your faith in that order that you did not consider the possibility of failure. In following the order of guru there is no failure. A follower of the spiritual master has no separate existence. You did not take time out of your personal life to do something for your spiritual master: your life *was* the order of your spiritual master. You never felt that you were alone: Kṛṣṇa and your spiritual master were always with you, and your movement—your branch of the Caitanya tree—was a solid fact. As you said to an acquaintance in New York as early as 1965: “There are temples and books. They are existing, they are there, but the time is separating us from them.”

*śrī-guru-caraṇa-padma, kevala bhakati-sadma,*  
*bandoṅ mui sāvadhāna mate*

**“The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way by which we can obtain pure devotional service. I bow down to his lotus feet with great awe and reverence”**

The Vedic literature describes many yogic perfections, but the ability to reach out and touch a person’s heart and transform that person’s every desire into the pure desire for service to the Lord—this is the highest mystic perfection of the pure *bhakti-yogī*.

We do not fully understand Kṛṣṇa, we do not fully understand what it means to be a devotee, what it means to be a *rūpānuga,* but we have you. Please let us be your Prabhupādānugas.

You have given us Kṛṣṇa in the form of the holy name, in the pages of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* in the Deity form you’ve allowed us to worship, and in the association of the devotees. You have entrusted Lord Caitanya’s movement into our hands. You have given us the *sādhana*-*bhakti* process that can actually elevate us to the steady, realized platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And you’ve bestowed all these gifts by your mercy

You have given so many instructions, and when we take these instructions to heart and make a firm commitment to follow them, we are sure to one day taste the fruit of perfection in *bhakti-yoga*.

Our village community here is a small but diverse group of devotees of all ages. Our project has gone through different stages, beginning as an outpost of enthusiastic pioneers, dealing with change and adversity, and transforming from a farm community into a village. Throughout it all we have had your instructions and the association of your followers. We believe that this is our *prabhu-datta deśa,* the place where our service is pleasing to the Lord, accessible to the *jīva* souls in this Kali-yuga, and useful to your ISKCON movement.

Please help us take full shelter of your instructions, making them our very life and soul. Please help us enter deeper into the relationship of servants to our masters—to you, our dear spiritual father and ever well-wisher, and to our Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, especially in His forms as Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu and the holy name. Please help us appreciate all the different devotees and take full advantage of their association. Please help us find ways to include and accept all the devotees serving you together with respect and love. Please let us participate actively in Lord Caitanya’s preaching mission. Please give us your mercy and be with us as we continue in our service, working to bring out the full potency of our New Caitanya Candrodaya temple and village community as it unfolds and blossoms, becoming a living example of your teachings in so many wonderful ways.

Aspiring for the shelter of your lotus feet, we remain  
Your humble servants at the New Caitanya Candrodaya Mandira, Almviks Gard, Jarna, Sweden.  
(written by Labaṅgalatikā Sevinī Devī Dāsī)

## New Gokula (Australia)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You are the personification of kindness and mercy, sent here by Their Lordships to liberate the whole world.

As ordinary, conditioned souls struggling within the material energy, we had no chance of lifting ourselves out of this ocean of ignorance and suffering. But your instructions have opened our eyes with the torchlight of knowledge, knowledge that only continues to grow as we read your books, delivered to us in the unadulterated disciplic succession, the Brahma-Madhva-Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava *sampradāya*.

As members of the New Gokula farm community here in Australia, we understand that it is our duty to please you—and thus satisfy Their Lordships—by creating a working model of an agrarian community based on the principles enunciated by Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself, a community in which everyone can work according to the nature the Supreme Lord has bestowed upon them and at the same time make spiritual progress. This ideal is supported by these statements of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s from the *Bhagavad-gītā:*

By following his qualities of work, every man can become perfect. Now please hear from Me how this can be done. By worship of the Lord, who is the source of all beings and who is all-pervading, a man can attain perfection through performing his own work. It is better to engage in one’s own occupation, even though one may perform it imperfectly, than to accept another’s occupation and perform it perfectly. Duties prescribed according to one’s nature are never affected by sinful reactions. Every endeavor is covered by some fault, just as fire is covered by smoke. Therefore one should not give up the work born of his nature, O son of Kuntī, even if such work is full of fault. \[*Bhagavad-gītā* 18.45–48\]

Though engaged in all kinds of activities, My devotee, under My protection, reaches the eternal and imperishable abode by My grace. \[*Bhagavad-gītā* 18.56\]

In addition, Lord *Brahmā* says this to Mahārāja Priyavrata at *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 5.1.14:

*yad-vāci tantyāṁ guṇa-karma-dāmabhiḥ*  
*sudustarair vatsa vayaṁ suyojitāḥ*  
*sarve vahāmo balim īśvarāya*  
*protā nasīva dvi-pade catuṣ-padaḥ*

**“My dear boy, all of us are bound by the Vedic injunctions to the divisions of** ***varṇāśrama*** **according to our qualities and work. These divisions are difficult to avoid because they are scientifically arranged. We must therefore carry out our duties of** ***varṇāśrama-dharma,*** **like bulls obliged to move according to the direction of a driver pulling on ropes knotted to their noses.”**

\[From Śrīla Prabhupāda’s purport:\] The *varṇāśrama* system is scientific. Therefore if we accept the divisions of *varṇa* and *āśrama* according to the Vedic instructions, our lives will be successful. Unless human society is thus divided and arranged, it cannot be perfect. As stated in the *Viṣṇu Purāṇa* (3.8.9):

*varṇāśramācāravatā puruṣeṇa paraḥ pumān*  
*viṣṇur ārādhyate panthā nānyat tat-toṣa-kāraṇam*

**“The Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Viṣṇu, is worshiped by the proper execution of prescribed duties in the system of** ***varṇa*** **and** ***āśrama*****. There is no other way to satisfy the Supreme Personality of Godhead. One must be situated in the institution of the four** ***varṇas*** **and** ***āśramas*****.” . . .**

The injunctions in the *Vedas* are explicit directions from the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Over the years since you left our mortal vision, dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, your movement has gone through many upheavals. Where once there was a thriving community in Millfield, with many industrious and enthusiastic devotees, we now have only a few dedicated souls.

In the past much suffering has occurred all over the ISKCON world due to a lack of understanding of our actual natures and the human condition. Proper understanding was lacking, along with proper compassion for the innocent. Even though we knew and directly experienced that you were nothing like us, when we saw how easily you performed pure *bhakti* we thought (like complete idiots) that we could emulate you, forgetting just how fallen we were and how it was only by your empowerment that we were able to push on Lord Caitanya’s message of chanting the holy names. You were never from this world! You are an eternal associate of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. All the twenty-six qualities of a pure Vaiṣṇava are manifest resplendently within you. Fortunately, by your mercy, we are now armed with a greater spiritual maturity and are determined not to repeat the mistakes of the past.

At New Gokula we are striving to please you by creating a sanctuary for both visitors and permanent residents, a sanctuary where all worship Their beautiful Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda; Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and *Śrī*matī Subhadrā; and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and where all live in harmony with mother nature and the cows while joyfully performing their duties and keeping Kṛṣṇa at the center of all activities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books are the basis, and if we develop our understanding of both the pure philosophy and the practical advice they contain, we can, by your divine grace, achieve success.

Great inroads into society at large have been made by the many dedicated souls who have distributed your books over the years, both in person and by making them so easily accessible online. We pray we may be among those who further contribute by creating a working model of a caring Kṛṣṇa conscious community, as described within these transcendental literatures.

Your humble servants at ISKCON’S New Gokula Farm, Millfield, New South Wales, Australia.

## New Gokula (Canada)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our collective obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories unto you, our loving master, father, grandsire, and guide.

It has been thirty-seven years since you disappeared from our mortal vision. Most of us in ISKCON nowadays never experienced your physical presence, beheld your beautiful form, witnessed live your miraculous and unprecedented feats, or heard the transcendental sound coming directly from your lotus mouth. Strangely enough, however, these common limitations appear not to have limited your ability to extend your mercy. As with our previous *ācāryas,* your mercy extends far beyond material time and space, to any and all who avail themselves of your *vāṇī* form with due submission and sincere aural reception. Śrīla Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda has stated: “The souls of the great thinkers of the bygone ages, who now live spiritually, often approach our inquiring spirit and assist it in its development.” Your Divine Grace not only assists us in our inquiry—you nourish our very soul.

During the last decade of your manifest *līlā* you flew here and there, transforming hearts amid the concrete walls of skyscrapers in the streets of big Western cities, on the islands within the great seas and oceans, in tropical jungles, and deep in the Canadian wilderness forests, where, somehow, by your miracle, Vaiṣṇavas now hear and chant *harināma* together. You leapt like *Hanumān* with his tail ablaze, setting fire to the entire world and igniting a global inferno of *harināma*-*saṅkīrtana*. Thus you were singularly and especially empowered by Śrī *Nityānanda* Prabhu Himself to propel the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu out of the confines of India and across the face of the earth, giving her much-needed hope and joy.

In the years since you entered your unmanifest *līlā,* the momentum generated from the tsunami wave of your mercy, which washed over seven vast continents, has continued to reverberate, striking fear into the hearts of atheist cheaters and attracting the hearts and minds of all sincere, yearning souls. This lasting effect is further proof of your special mercy and unique power.

As we all know, crisis after crisis has come upon us, and the grand material *māyā* has tested our determination to serve your mission, which is her eternal duty. For the damage done, we have only ourselves to blame, as you have already delivered to us all means of success. It is thus up to us to employ them in our lives. You have given everything in your books, yet who among us studies them deeply? Those who do can see the direction your divine finger points toward. Acting on that direction, those devotees are grateful and feel indebted to your lotus feet:

*nāma-śreṣṭhaṁ manum api śacī-putram.*

We are fully indebted to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are giving us so many things. You are giving us the holy name, containing the highest form of thought, aspiration, and ideal, and you are giving us the service of that great savior, the son of Mother Śacī, Śrī Caitanya Mahaprabhu, who is like a golden mountain indicating the way to *kṛṣṇa*-*līlā*. (Paraphrased from Śrīla Raghunātha Dāsa *Gosvāmī*’s *Muktā-carita*)
