This untainted message and current of love have come down through realized transmission in *paramparā* from Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, to Śrīla *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī*, and through his *rūpānuga* *guru-varga* line to you, and through your divine agency, to us. Armed and supported by your army of protectors, how then could we ever become vulnerable or experience diminished faith in the power of *mahā*-*prasadam*, Śrī Govinda and His holy name, the *brāhmaṇas,* and genuine Vaiṣṇavas?

Sometimes we ask ourselves why it is that after so many years of serving we feel meager progress in our spiritual lives. We often engage in idle talk, worry for our material stability, and calculate our way into old-age security. Abandoning the hope of *śaraṇāgati,* we lose confidence. With the passage of time, we more often ask “What can the temple do for me?” than “What can I do for the temple?” Meanwhile, we wonder why the higher stages of *bhakti* have eluded us while our *anarthas* remain alive and even flourish within us. “Was the promise of attaining *bhāva* a false one?” we ask. “Does the storehouse of love of God even exist, what to speak of being plundered and distributed freely to all recipients without concern for who is qualified or not?” These doubts are all nonsense and the products of faithlessness arising from our own misconceptions of ourselves as the “doers” and “achievers” of our own destinies. In *bhakti,* we are dependent on the mercy of those who are dependent on *Śrī* Guru.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa Himself has stated in the *Ādi* *Purāṇa*:

*ye me bhakta-janāḥ pārtha na me bhaktāś ca te janāḥ*  
*mad-bhaktānāṁ ca ye bhaktās te me bhakta-tamā matāḥ*

***“*****Those who are My direct devotees are not actually My devotees, but those who are the devotees of My devotees are My actual devotees.”**

Therefore, just as simply by being exposed to sunshine we receive illumination, warmth, and life, simply by properly associating with *sādhus* we will receive their mercy. With the Vaiṣṇavas’ mercy, a very sweet and humble mood appears within the heart of the recipient. We will pray to Harināma Prabhu, the *śāstra,* and the *dhāma*, but we will pray only for further taste in *harināma*. Thus, good results will appear within us. As you have stated, “The result of chanting is that it leads to more chanting.”

We have heard so many instructions, but if we have no relationship with Nāma Prabhu or His Vaiṣṇava devotees, our hearts will never change. Nothing can be pushed into a heart made of stone. “Better to tear down the marble from the walls and sell it for the printing and distribution of books.”

What constitutes favorable Vaiṣṇava-saṅga has been stated sweetly in Śrīla *Rūpa* *Gosvāmī*’s *Nectar of Instruction*, verse 4:

Offering gifts in charity, accepting charitable gifts, revealing one’s mind in confidence, inquiring confidentially, accepting *prasādam,* and offering *prasādam* are the six symptoms of love shared by one devotee and another.

These exchanges are found exclusively among those Vaiṣṇavas whom we ever aspire to emulate. All success arises within the practitioner of *bhakti* when the quality of those exchanges pleases the Lord, who then bestows His mercy. Facilitating and culturing such relationships constitute the purpose of having temples in ISKCON; that devotional character blossoms in the persona of the devotees. The results are infectious, and sincere souls are inevitably drawn to an atmosphere of devotional enthusiasm and energy. Money and facilities then follow of their own accord, like faithful servants, and they need not be separately endeavored for on the basis of some material calculation or nefarious scheme.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are utterly dependent upon you for our success in this human form. If not for you, we would still be out there, alone and afraid, exhausted from treading the cold, dark waters of material existence. Please help us recognize genuine Vaiṣṇavas and help us relate to them appropriately, according to their devotional disposition (*adhikāra*). Above all else, please give us the strength, your mercy potency, to enable us to truly become Vaiṣṇavas and serve you in a mood free from material motives based on some weird misconception of ourselves, the Lord, or His energies. Thus we can advance further in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and give you pleasure.

Your humble servants at New Gokula Dhāma, ISKCON Vancouver, Canada.

## New Govardhana (USA)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of** ***Bhaktisiddhānta*** **Sarasvatī** ***Gosvāmī*****. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I am deeply honored to have the opportunity to write this offering to you on behalf of the New Govardhana community in San Diego, California. Despite my limitations, I can only attempt to convey the profound effect you have had and continue to have on my life and the lives of the other devotees of New Govardhana.

I was raised in a devout Roman Catholic family, and as a young boy I aspired to become a priest. But my many unresolved questions and negative experiences with the Church eventually turned my aspirations to apathy toward all things labeled “religion.” A verse in the Spanish version of the Penitential Rite is meant to be the proper response of a good Catholic to his or her suffering in this world. It states *Por mi culpa, por mi culpa, por my gran culpa!* (“Through my fault, through my fault, through my most grievous fault!”) I remember asking myself, “What did I do?” and “Why is it my fault?” These were among a list of questions I had that I never received satisfactory answers for. As a result, I stopped accompanying my mother to Mass and distanced myself from God.

But my desire to explore spirituality never wavered. So I dabbled in astrology, Native American rites, traditional Aztec beliefs, and *haṭha-yoga*. It was through *haṭha-yoga* that I learned of *bhakti-yoga,* and since I hadn’t exactly found what I was looking for, I researched it and enrolled in an introductory *bhakti-yoga* course here at New Govardhana. It was during this course that I finally got satisfying answers to the many questions I had about the soul and God. Within a few months my strained relationship with God had healed, and now it has become my most important relationship. With folded hands I thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this amazing gift! Thank you for mending my broken heart and for reconnecting this lowly fool with God in a way that fills my life with purpose and meaning. My experience, however, is not unique, as there are countless devotees with similar experiences.

All the devotees here in the New Govardhana community are eternally grateful for your mercy. Despite the many hardships you faced, you selflessly and bravely persevered. Your resilience and spiritual fortitude in bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the West and taking it all over the world was an act of pure love. A spiritual revolutionary, you have served as a catalyst to bring about a revolution in the consciousness of the conditioned souls everywhere. Almost forty years after you left this world, Kṛṣṇa consciousness continues to spread. New temples are being built, older ones are being modernized, and the ISKCON family is growing.

New Govardhana continues to be a vibrant spiritual oasis, with increasing potential and many opportunities for devotional service. By your mercy, over the last few years the number of regular home programs has increased throughout San Diego county. In addition, the temple has established a planning committee of devotees to support New Govardhana’s board of directors. These initiatives are being led by senior devotees whom you trained and whom many of us have the honor of serving. Please bless these initiatives so that we may assist you in spreading Lord Caitanya’s teachings throughout the world.

Your servants at New Govardhana Dhāma, San Diego, California, USA.  
(written by Bhakta Otto)

## New Hastināpura

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, spiritual master, savior, eternal guide, ever well-wisher, best masterful friend, strict representative of Śrīla Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī* Prabhupāda, founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, greatest exponent of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the Western world, general in the *saṅkīrtana* army of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, bringer of the authorized conclusions of Śrī *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu and the Six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana (headed by Śrī *Rūpa* and Śrī Sanātana), giver of the treasure of the *Bhagavad-gītā* *As It Is,* carrier of the mercy of Lord *Nityānanda* Prabhu, Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead, lord and master of the devotees, dearmost servant of Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī*, eternal resident of Goloka Vṛndāvana, and our only shelter—please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this anniversary of your most kind appearance in this world, we, your servants in Potomac, Maryland, are praying to remember your transcendental qualities. Śrīla Prabhupāda, in 1965, when you began your preaching in America, by your perfect character you attracted many sincere persons to assist you in establishing the mission of Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī* Prabhupāda and Lord *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu. Then you personally cared for your new disciples with great attention and kindness. Receiving your transcendental affection, they became enthused to serve your mission.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, four decades later we still feel your personal presence. Your unconditional love and clear instructions enliven us. Although you left this world almost thirty-seven years ago, your spiritual influence continuously increases, carried by this bond of love and broadcast everywhere by your divine instructions. Whenever we work to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we feel your presence within our hearts. You asked us to make strenuous efforts to expand the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world. But you yourself always set the example by working harder than any of us. You showered us with encouragement, love, discipline, proper instruction, and very high expectations. These gifts shelter us, guide us, and thus maintain us.

Gurudeva, you have given us a divine family of devotees who are our very life and soul. When we see these devotees, whether novice or stalwart, we immediately feel your presence. They are the fruits of your preaching work, and our spiritual advancement is measured by how much we appreciate them, even if we sometimes have different opinions about important matters.

The ISKCON society you created by your pure-hearted devotion is a spiritual shelter. We are grateful to have this shelter in the form of the association of many sincere devotees of the Lord, and in the form of the blissful preaching activities that sustain our spiritual lives.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in Washington, D.C., your book distribution has dramatically increased in the last ten years. Regular *harināma* *saṅkīrtana* parties have expanded, and thousands of people chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* in temple festivals, receive *prasādam,* hear the *Bhagavad-gītā,* and serve the Deities of Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Madana-Mohana, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, and Śrī Śrī Sītā-Rāma-Lakṣmaṇa-Hanumān. The devotees are increasingly realizing that spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness outside our community causes Kṛṣṇa consciousness to spread within our own hearts. The best way to honor, love, and serve someone who has left this world is to carry out the mission to which that person dedicated his life. Your mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda, was to alleviate the suffering of all the people of the world by teaching them how to reach the soothing shelter of Lord Nityānanda’s lotus feet. You tolerated many difficulties to spread this mission, and you have ordered us to continue your work.

\[S\] imply work cooperatively for spreading this movement all over the world. The standards I have already given you, now try to maintain them at all times under standard procedure. Do not try to innovate or create anything or manufacture anything, that will ruin everything. Simply do as I am doing and be always serious and sincere to serve Krsna, and He will give you intelligence how to do everything. \[letter to Bali Mārdana and Puṣṭa Kṛṣṇa, 18 September 1972\]

You remind us to tolerate difficulties, put aside our differences, and strive to appreciate one another and help one another serve Lord *Caitanya* Mahāprabhu. Please remain with us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your divine instructions. Kindly purify us, deliver us, and thus maintain us.

*ohe!*  
*vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, doyāra sāgara,*  
*e dāse korunā kori’*  
*diyā pada-chāyā, śodho he āmāya,*  
*tomāra caraṇa dhori*

*Your servants at* New Hastināpura, ISKCON Potomac, Washington, D.C., USA.  
*(*written by Lakṣmīvān Dāsa)

## New Jagannātha Purī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, O Jagat Guru, savior of the whole world!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we fallen followers of yours in your New Jagannātha Purī *dhāma* are happily remembering you on this, your auspicious appearance day. Here in New Jagannātha Purī we are so fortunate to have the first and oldest Deities of Lord Jagannātha, Lord Balarāma, and *Śrī*matī Subhadrā in the Western world. Their carving was commissioned by you, and you personally installed them in San Francisco in 1967. In that year you also initiated the first Ratha-yātrā in the Western World, in San Francisco’s Golden Gate Park. If not for your mercy and that of all the Vaiṣṇavas, we would not have been successful, but by your grace we will be holding the forty-eighth annual Ratha-yātrā this year. We are so grateful for this service and hope to continue it in years to come, in keeping with Lord *Caitanya*’s mission.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you told us that just by chanting “Hare Kṛṣṇa!” we would be happy, and every day we are seeing that this is true. So, in honor of your topmost desire, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are still going out on *harināma* every day, because we know that this is what you want us to do. By your grace we have come to the realization that everything you instructed us to do is perfect and the ultimate goal of our life and of our very existence.

In 2017 we will celebrate the Golden Anniversary of Ratha-yātrā in the Western world. We are beginning to prepare for this grand celebration and will attempt to make it a major event in ISKCON. We ask your blessings to make this a grand festival, one of the biggest that ISKCON has ever presented, with devotees coming from all over the world and people coming by the thousands to see your legacy on the 50th anniversary of Śrī Jagannātha Ratha-yātrā here in New Jagannātha Purī.

We beg to remain  
Your servants in New Jagannātha Purī, Berkeley, California, USA.  
(written by Jagannātha Swami Dāsa)

## New Māyāpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace! *Śrīla Prabhupāda*, you are the most wonderful devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa—a genuine well-wisher and savior of humanity at large—and you have captured our heart and soul.

New Māyāpur is inseparably connected with you. Here we always feel your personal *śakti,* and this feeling is also experienced regularly by visiting devotees. You forever lead us from your *vyāsāsana*, facing your Deities: the all-merciful Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, after whom you named this *dhāma* New Māyāpur; powerful Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, who you said would “give strength to the devotees and the movement in Europe”; and beautiful Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Govinda-Mādhava, whom you allowed us to install “later.” Your apartments in the “palace,” which you “liked very much” and where you translated your books, vibrate with your personal presence. In fact, everywhere in New Māyāpur we can remember you, spiritually guiding the devotees, organizing this project, and preaching to the guests. Beautifully sitting on the grass in these “unique outdoors,” surrounded by happy devotees who had come from all over Europe, you gave your *sūtra*-like vision for New Māyāpur: “A perfect place for loving Kṛṣṇa (*man-manā bhava mad-bhakto*) without difficulty (*yoga-kṣemaṁ vahāmy aham*), and a shelter for many in the future.”

As everyone knows, France is a charming country, but it has been strongly polluted by sense gratification and atheistic philosophy. Thus for Europe it has historically been the cradle of a sterile and destructive revolutionary spirit, which is still alive today. This year, once again, the European Parliament rejected with concern a proposal by the French government to extend to all Europe its repressive laws against spiritual minorities. However, you mercifully did not neglect this difficult country, which you visited five times. Now in all of continental Europe this temple stands as the only one left from your time, like a great family home for ISKCON devotees. And just as you instructed that the Temple of the Vedic Planetarium, the greatest challenge to modern materialistic science, be established in Śrīdhām Māyāpur, ISKCON’s world headquarters, so you settled New Māyāpur, a big challenge to godlessness, in France, at the heart of atheism.

Today, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we—your humble, poor, and aging servants—beg you to bless our renewed endeavors to maintain and preach for your New Māyāpur embassy according to your instructions, so that future generations can benefit from this wonderful and potent spiritual oasis, of which you are the founder-*ācārya*.

Your grateful servants in New Māyāpur, France.

## New Navadvīpa Dhāma

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”

Please accept our most respectful, humble obeisances.

On behalf of all the devotees of New Navadvīpa Dhāma in Hawaii, we wish to express our gratitude to you for giving us the opportunity to participate in Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission. Without your grace it would have been impossible for us to understand the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Without your compassionate mercy it would have been impossible for us to participate in this divine mission. Here in Hawaii, you first established the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in late 1968 and early 1969, and since that time many devotees have become your followers because only you can show us the path back home, back to Godhead.

*(yadi) prabhupāda nā haita tabe ki haita*  
*(e) jivana bahita kise?*  
*nitāi-gaurera apāra karuṇa*  
*ke dita sakala deśe*

**“If Śrīla Prabhupāda had not come, what would have happened? How could we have passed our lives? Who else would have distributed the unlimited mercy of Lord** ***Nityānanda*** **and Lord** ***Gaurāṅga*** **all over the world?” (From a Bengali song by His Holiness Jayapatāka Swami)**

If you had not come to these Hawaiian Islands, what would have happened to fallen souls like us? But because of your mercy and inspiration, we continue to carry out your order to push on Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission. It is our privilege to make a commitment to satisfy Your Divine Grace.

Under your guidance, every Saturday evening we go out on *harināma* *saṅkīrtana* in Waikiki Beach, Hawaii’s international tourist capital. We worship the celebrated Śrī Śrī Pañca-tattva Deities, which you personally installed. We engage in book and *prasādam* distribution and faithfully celebrate all the festivals you have instructed us to. Additionally, we also continue the weekly tradition of the Sunday Love Feast.

Your Divine Grace, we petition you to bless our new projects, which will create new opportunities for the residents of the Hawaiian Islands to participate in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Because you are our spiritual master and hope, we approach you with humility. We understand that without your causeless divine mercy we cannot be successful in any of our endeavors. Please, therefore, bless us so we can fulfill our desire to have a traveling *saṅkīrtana* bus party in the Hawaiian Islands.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg that you mercifully empower us to continue serving you at every moment of our lives. Here in New Navadvīpa *dhāma*, the devotees are very much looking forward to celebrating your auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā with great enthusiasm.

Your humble servants at ISKCON’s New Navadvīpa Dhāma, Honolulu, Hawaii, USA.  
(written by Sundarānanda Dāsa)

## New Nīlācala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

There have been changes at ISKCON of Philadelphia over the past year. Ravīndra Svarūpa Prabhu and his good wife Saudāmanī Devī Dāsī, who had been managing the center for over twenty years, retired from temple management and moved away. This change had been encouraged by the GBC so that Ravīndra Svarūpa Prabhu would have more time to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness through writing. Several of his disciples relocated with him. Because of these changes, there were concerns that the temple might not continue, since many essential services would not be covered and the financial situation was precarious. Some of us prayed to you for help and guidance. We also appealed to the Vaiṣṇava community for assistance.

Then an interesting thing happened. Many devotees in the area took the situation as an opportunity to increase their service to Lord Kṛṣṇa. We also received vital help from the GBC and devotees from Potomac and Atlanta. Śikhi Māhiti Prabhu has stepped in as our new temple president, and he’s been doing an exceptional job. The Deity services are covered, the festivals have been well attended, our financial situation has stabilized, and book distribution and other preaching efforts have continued and are developing.

All this could not have happened without your presence as the unifying, inspiring, and guiding force of ISKCON. You continue to provide the transcendental philosophy, laws, *sādhana,* devotees, vision, and personal example needed for cooperation and progress. The proof of this is that devotees coming from various backgrounds, with different *dīkṣā*-*gurus,* of different ages, and living in various locations have rallied to the cause of Kṛṣṇa’s devotional service in Philadelphia, as inspired by you.

You requested devotees to cooperate to push on the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We are happy to report that here in Philadelphia we are cooperating forty-eight years after you founded ISKCON.

The French are fond of saying *plus ca change plus c’est la meme chose* (“The more things change, the more they remain the same.”) This is relevant in our case, for despite so many changes, the whole menu of delightful devotional services to Kṛṣṇa continues here in Philadelphia.

We pray to your lotus feet to help us make ISKCON of Philadelphia an even more vibrant and welcoming center for broadcasting Lord Caitanya’s glories and message to the residents of the greater Philadelphia area, as exemplified by you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Thank you.

Your servants at New Nīlācala Dhāma, Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, USA.  
(written by Viṣṇugadā Dāsa)

## New Orleans

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In 1975 you placed your lotus feet in the city of New Orleans and arrived at our fledgling temple, at once creating a transcendental place of pilgrimage, for all time. The then mayor of the city, Ernest Morial, granted you the key to the city and opened all doors for you to traverse its environs and preach at will. And so you blessed New Orleans, “the city that care forgot,” and left an indelible impression upon it for all future generations of devotees and well-wishers, who have since brought so much Kṛṣṇa consciousness to a place that frankly has few rivals in the pursuit and promotion of the most bewildering phantasmagoria of hedonism.

Since that time mass book distribution has gone on nonstop, with some of the world’s top distributors pumping out steady streams of your saving grace to the multifarious droves of humanity who come here seeking elusive and illusory happiness. On occasion challengers have tried to revoke the carte blanche you were given for preaching, but your determined servants have fought tooth and nail and, by your mercy, always come away victorious, securing its continuance.

In the years following your historic and benedictory visit, the world-famous annual Mardi Gras festival in New Orleans regularly drew North America’s biggest *mahā-rathī saṅkīrtana* leaders, who never failed to bring Vaikuṇṭha to earth, creating roaring *kīrtanas* and distributing record-breaking numbers of your books to immense crowds of revelers, many of whom would fall right in to join our transcendentally joyous chanting party. Throughout it all we experienced your presence buoying up the crowds, floating down the packed, narrow streets, washing over the darkened atmosphere with the ecstatic light of your and Lord Caitanya’s inconceivable compassion, never touching the ground.

Those were the magnificent highlights of our days with you in New Orleans. As time passes and great *mahā*-*rathīs* come and go, as preaching forums change and evolve, you continue to give us the faith, the knowledge, the enthusiasm, and a fraction of your compassion to place the mercy of your divine words into the hands of the most unfortunate, as we try every day to share you with the world. The more we do so, the more tangible you become to us and the more we understand that we are nothing other than aspiring instruments of Your Divine Grace.

Please continue to bless us so that we may always remain with you as your humble servants, working to bring the cooling shade of your lotus feet to this parched, burning, squalid place on earth, so that one day it will cease to be known as the city that care forgot and will instead become known as the city that Kṛṣṇa could not forget.

Your servants in New Saṅkīrtana Purī, New Orleans, Louisiana, USA.

## New Pānihāṭi Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of** ***Bhaktisiddhānta*** **Sarasvatī** ***Gosvāmī*****. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord** ***Caitanya*** **and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

On this most glorious anniversary of your appearance, I can only think of how you sacrificed your home in Vṛndāvana and the comforts of being surrounded by the Vrajavāsīs and thousands of temples, and you came to America to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

As a second-generation aspiring servant of yours, when I look around I see nothing but the evidence of your presence in the many dedicated disciples you left behind for us, the thousands of second-generation devotees, and so many wonderful temples and communities where the holy name is vibrating through the ether and the spiritual energy is permeating far and wide. Your love for us is written all over the faces of your disciples, who have tasted that nectar of your love and care. Reading and hearing your disciples’ narrations of their association with you, I find that they were surrounded by your compassion and love and care, and I realize how fortunate they are. If we follow in their footsteps, there is hope for us to taste that nectar.

The past ten years I have been privileged to serve here in Atlanta, New Pānihāṭi Dhāma. I often remember your transcendental pastime of delivering your arrival address here with a choked voice, narrating the pastime of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mercy, saying that He doesn’t even require us to fully surrender. I remember how you took so much pain and hardship to give that mercy of Lord Caitanya’s to so many fallen, conditioned souls so that we would get a chance to taste it.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in New Pānihāṭi Dhāma we have been attempting to follow your instructions, with regular systematic study of your books, such as *Śrīmad* *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and weekly street *harināma* *saṅkīrtana* and *prasādam* distribution. I know that your mercy is in your instructions and that we can feel your presence in our lives only by following them. We beg that you please empower your lowly servants here to always follow your instructions and also to preach them so that we will always remain in your ISKCON.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have transformed the hearts of thousands with your mercy in the form of your books, with their wonderful purports, and we can feel your presence when we read your books and hear you speaking through them. You have given us everything we need to know; there is no need to go elsewhere. Please bless us that we may never stray from the shade of the dust of your lotus feet.

Your servants at New Pānihāṭi Dhāma, Atlanta, Georgia, USA.  
(written by Vedasāra Dāsa)

## New Ramaṇa Reti Dhāma

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*‘sādhu-saṅga’, ‘sādhu-saṅga’—sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

**“The verdict of all revealed scriptures is that by even a moment’s association with a pure devotee, one can attain all success. ” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā*** **22.54)**

One moment!  
One moment!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were and *are* so generous with your moments. Your disciples directly experienced the timeless ecstasy that surrounded you and enveloped them. You shared all the time in the world with us as you patiently guided us in service—administering the medicine, slowly but surely curing us of the disease of materialistic life. These moments are eternal. Everything else fades, pales in comparison. These moments continue to sustain us and take on a life of their own. Scintillating, pulsating, they take root in our heart, where they continue to nourish and inspire us.

The centers you established are still offering sanctuary to anyone who visits. Like oases in the desert, they beckon one and all to come and drink from the immortal fountain of mercy and knowledge.

Recently a godsister shared an experience she had had at a support meeting. There were only a few members at this particular meeting, and none of the others knew she was a devotee. One member, Sarah, began relating her experience of a traumatic car accident. Someone had blindsided her, and the impact caused her SUV to roll over. Her life had flashed before her eyes, and Sarah shared with the group that she couldn’t remember whether she was supposed to be thinking of Kṛṣṇa or Viṣṇu.

“What!!?” the devotee asked.

Sarah repeated, “I couldn’t remember whether I should be thinking of Kṛṣṇa or Viṣṇu.”

“Either would work,” the devotee said. “I’m a Hare Kṛṣṇa devotee,” she revealed. “How is it that you were remembering Kṛṣṇa and Viṣṇu?”

Sarah said, “Years ago I visited a Hare Kṛṣṇa temple, and I *never* forgot what they taught me.”

Confronted with death, she remembered the pure devotee’s essential instruction for anyone in that situation. One small story, but there are countless anecdotes like this.

The web of your mercy extends beyond our comprehension. Everywhere we see evidence of your presence and the changes your association have brought to our world.

Besides the personal transformations your disciples have undergone, the whole world continues to undergo spiritual transformation due to the moments of your association. Vegetarianism was something relegated to a few eccentric beatniks back in the day, but presently you can request vegetarian entrees on any airline and in most restaurants. This is a result of your divine presence. *Prasādam* and your books are purifying the world. *Prasādam* distribution is one of the most effective ways that your disciples and granddisciples distribute Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. I remember you lecturing underneath a banyan tree on a balmy evening in Miami in 1975. Speaking of *prasādam,* you said that all of us had come to you due to *prasādam*. “Even you, Abhirāma,” you quipped, glancing at our temple president. *Prasādam,* the ultimate weapon, continues to work imperceptibly but powerfully to transform the world. Today devotees widely distribute *prasādam* on college campuses and wherever and whenever else they have an opportunity. As the *prasādam* works its magic, countless souls will come to your lotus feet in time.

*Kīrtana* was virtually unheard-of before you came to America in 1965, but by your strong desire and tireless efforts *kīrtana* is becoming a household word, and the world is thus undergoing extensive purification. There’s a YouTube video of a Catholic woman who was invited to the home of one of her students, an incognito Hare Kṛṣṇa, for a yoga experience. She didn’t realize she was in the middle of a Hare Kṛṣṇa *kīrtana* until it was too late! She was transformed! *Harināma-saṅkīrtana* has experienced a renaissance worldwide. All living entities in Kali-yuga are always close to tragedy and disaster. You told us that the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa could turn back the tide of Kali-yuga. It’s the only way, the only way, the only way. Ratha-yātrā festivals continue to expand worldwide—a dream of yours from childhood. People still approach us who are unfamiliar with the festival and ask, “What is this?” They beg to know more. Everyone joins in the labor of love, pulling the cart with Their Lordships seated regally, mercifully guiding the celebrants, as Lord Jagannātha floods the world with His mercy.

In 1976 in Vṛndāvana I awoke early and decided to walk near your quarters and chant my rounds. It was about 1:30 a.m., and I could see you through the window, painstakingly translating your books, knowing that they were the only hope for our pathetic world. I stood and watched you, aware that I was observing some unique moments. Stories of your books liberating the conditioned souls abound. “These books act like Deities,” you explained. “If they read one word their lives will be changed forever. Even if someone purchases a book and places it on the bookshelf, the book will act, purifying the home.” One devotee told me how she purchased a book at the Honolulu airport with no intention of ever reading it. She brought it home and placed it on an upper shelf, and there it sat for eight years purifying her home. One day while she sat daydreaming she looked up, saw the book, took it off the shelf, and began to read it. Convinced, she went to the nearest temple and surrendered.

Your genius is unfathomable, your compassion incomprehensible. The quintessential miracle is the ongoing power of your *prasādam, kīrtana,* and books. Drops of mercy, unconditional mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in a poem you wrote onboard the *Jaladuta* in 1965, entitled “Prayer unto the Lotus Feet of Kṛṣṇa” you spoke of the desire of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura:

*tāra icchā balavān pāścātyete ṭhān ṭhān*  
*hoy jāte gaurāṅger nām*  
*pṛthivīte nagarādi āsamudra nada nadī*  
*sakalei loy kṛṣṇa nām*

**“By \[Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta** ***Sarasvatī*****’s\] strong desire, the holy name of Lord** ***Gaurāṅga*** **will spread throughout the countries of the Western world. In all the cities, towns, and villages on the earth, from all the oceans, seas, rivers, and streams, everyone will chant the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you worked tirelessly to make sure Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s words were not in vain.

We pray to become *your* instruments—your pure instruments. You have set the stage and already accomplished the mission. Still, out of your mercy you are giving us an opportunity to participate in fulfilling Lord *Caitanya*’s prediction. This movement will travel to every town, village, and farm. That is a fact. If we don’t take up the task, others will. Please empower us; purify our hearts so that our only desire will be to push on this *saṅkīrtana* movement.

When the time comes for us to receive your personal association once again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will honor that moment. Inspired by a verse spoken by Lord Caitanya and recorded in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya* 2.38), we declare:

*punaḥ yadi kona kṣaṇa, karāya prabhupāda daraśana*  
*tabe sei ghaṭī-kṣaṇa-pala*  
*diyā mālya-candana, nānā ratna-ābharaṇa,*  
*alaṅkṛta karimu sakala*

**“If by chance such a moment comes when I can once again see Śrīla Prabhupāda, then I shall worship those seconds, moments, and hours with flower garlands and pulp of sandalwood and decorate them with all kinds of jewels and ornaments.” Until that blessed reunion occurs,** ***Śrīla Prabhupāda*****, please allow us to deliver your unconditional mercy.**

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your unconditional love.

Your devoted servants at New Ramaṇa Reti Dhāma, ISKCON’s rural community in Alachua, Florida, USA.  
(written by Sukhadā Devī Dāsī)

## New Remuṇā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this glorious anniversary of your appearance, we offer our most heartfelt thanks to you for the many gifts you have bestowed on us and all your sincere followers. The devotees of ISKCON Toronto, New Remuṇā Dhāma, would like to humbly present the small services we have rendered to your lotus feet, with the hope that we can continue to receive your mercy and shelter as we try to gain a small taste for devotional service to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the world around us today promotes itself as technologically robust and laden with scientific and industrial advances. Especially in the West, people live in a world where the release of a new computer or portable phone is celebrated as if it were the arrival of messengers from heaven. The public is constantly bombarded with advertisements that induce them to consume more products, with the promise to help increase the comfort of their daily lives. Even something as simple as going to the grocery store to buy some produce is now discussed among business strategists and psychologists so as to make the consumer’s “shopping experience” more pleasurable, comfortable, and convenient. The purpose of all this, of course, is to get the public to spend more of their hard-earned money in the futile attempt to buy happiness. Materialism and hedonism are rampant, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and the scariest part is that they are cloaked in a shiny, attractive, alluring package to convince the masses that happiness can be found through the almighty dollar.

As you aptly state in your purport to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 7.14.10:

At the present moment, however, everyone is engaged in technological advancement, which is described in *Bhagavad-gītā* as *ugra-karma*—extremely severe endeavor. This *ugra-karma* is the cause of agitation within the human mind. Men are engaging in many sinful activities and becoming degraded by opening slaughterhouses, breweries, and cigarette factories, as well as nightclubs and other establishments for sense enjoyment. In this way they are spoiling their lives.

Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, depression rates are at all-time highs. Reports of mental illnesses continue to rise. The unbridled breakdown of the family unit and the destruction of the very social fabric that people rely on continue unabated. Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is for these reasons, and more, that we find a continued rising interest in Kṛṣṇa consciousness here in Toronto. In fact, although we are in the heart of materialism in downtown Toronto, our temple continues to flourish. Whether it be an eye-opening conversation on the street when distributing books, giving the holy name to students as they stress out preparing for exams, or teaching a yoga practitioner how to chant on beads, the devotees in our community continue to see that there is an eager appetite for Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the last year was a tumultuous one for the devotees of ISKCON Toronto. As a large community of devotees, we faced challenges together, seized opportunities together, broke new ground together, and collectively grew together in the service of Śrī Śrī *Rādhā*Kṣīra-cora Gopīnātha and you.

In our temple, grand festivals throughout the year saw people flood into our building to take *darśana* of the Deities and enthusiastically celebrate through *kīrtana,* classes, dramas, *prasādam,* and more. We continue to see wonderful turnouts for our Sunday Feasts and are inspired with the challenge of how to create more personal connections with our guests to help introduce them to your books and chanting the holy name. Our dedicated *pūjārīs* continue to render service to our beloved Deities with great care and attention to detail. The population of our temple *āśrama* has continued to ebb and flow, and we are pressing the search for a model by which we can develop a thriving *āśrama* for both men and women. Our old building continues to present new challenges, and we are always on our toes, trying to keep up with the ongoing needs of our historic home, which you personally selected back in 1975. We are developing grand plans to care for and utilize our temple building in the best possible fashion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, outreach continues to thrive here at ISKCON Toronto through the tireless efforts of devotees who pour seemingly unlimited amounts of time into their service. The Kṛṣṇa Club program at York University regularly sees keenly inquisitive students attend programs where they are introduced to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Book distribution, something so close to your heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda, expanded this past year, and we set and achieved our goals as devotees distributed tens of thousands of your books. Monthly *saṅkīrtana* festivals are now just one of many ways that your books are making their way from the boxes in our storage room to the hands and minds of spiritual seekers.

Every month we hold an “Evening of Bhakti” at the temple, and the crowds are swelling as the yoga community continues to take notice that the Hare Kṛṣṇa temple is a hotspot for *kīrtana,* delicious food, and philosophy. Through dedicated effort and investment, our Govinda’s restaurant has steadily grown, and we are seeing countless people come to your temple to partake of a dining experience that is so satisfying that they continue to visit time and time again. Our Ratha-yātrā has continued its explosive growth, with tens of thousands coming to take part in festivities that now extend beyond the weekend festival. The dynamic approach of bringing the Ratha-yātrā back to an outreach focus is seeing incredible success, with media, corporate sponsors, and masses of people from varied ethnic backgrounds lining up to chant and learn of our spiritual culture. Every month our Food for Life program continues to serve hot *prasādam* to Toronto’s homeless, and *kīrtana* always accompanies the *prasādam* as it is handed out. We preformed a massive *harināma* at City Hall on New Year’s Eve, with thousands of revelers joining the devotees in dancing for joy. Several times a year, thousands upon thousands of convention-goers line up to buy *prasādam* at wonderfully successful food fairs. It is through these humble efforts that we continue to seek your blessings and guidance as we try to boldly share your message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness with the entire city.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as a growing community, we have begun to see the need to take care of our devotees. Cultivation efforts continue through wonderful initiatives like Bhakti Vṛkṣas, personal mentoring, and the building of friendships, but we are seeing the pressing need to formalize and develop programs to care for those who are newer in their practice of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We see this as a monumental challenge, and, as a community, we hope to realize the full potential of mentoring and caring for these newer devotees. Great strides are being made in developing formal educational programs for our community through seminars, classes, and workshops, and we continue to see a strong thirst for knowledge of your books and teachings from our devotee community.

The bright faces and squealing laughter of children resonate both at exciting Kids Camps and also throughout our temple as the now-formalized Sunday School program continues to blossom, resulting in children regaling their parents with pastimes of Kṛṣṇa as families drive home from the temple after Sunday Feasts. We are also humbly continuing to build bridges between our temple and the three other thriving centers in the Toronto area—ISKCON Scarborough, ISKCON Brampton, and the Bhakti Lounge. Our community of devotees at ISKCON Toronto has taken notice of the growing wave of enthusiasm in our movement with respect to devotee care. Our sincere desire is to develop programs, ideas, and support systems for our own community of devotees during their times of need. Whether it is bringing *prasādam* to a devotee who is ill, or offering spiritual and emotional support for devotee families when they lose loved ones, we see the growing need to take care of our own community, and we seek your mercy and blessings as we make small steps toward this goal.

Central to making all this happen is the guidance and blessings of senior Vaiṣṇavas. We feel truly blessed, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to have the personal association of so many of your direct disciples and granddisciples, who take time out of their busy schedules to come to New Remuṇā Dhāma. Senior devotees like Vaiśeṣika Prabhu, Saṅkarṣaṇa Prabhu, Laxmīmonī Dāsī, Bhakti Bṛhat Bhāgavata Swami, Draviḍa Prabhu, Devāmṛta Swami, Kṛṣṇa-nandinī Dāsī, *Nityānanda* Prabhu, and so many more become personifications of inspiration as they bring your teachings and instructions to the hungry devotees of Toronto. Combining this with the ongoing guidance we receive from both Bhaktimārga Swami and Gopāl Kṛṣṇa Goswami yields a community that continually feels blanketed in love, care, and affection from senior Vaiṣṇavas.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are clearly the smallest of saplings in the gargantuan forest of worldwide devotees, and we do not feel qualified to render service to your lotus feet. It will only be through your blessings that we will be able to see our community establish stronger roots, branch out to wider audiences, and bear the sweetest of fruits in the form of soft-hearted devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the message of materialism will certainly continue to bombard the wider populace, and it will do so with the mirage of shimmering images, dazzling tricks, and fancy packaging. But with your mercy, the devotees of ISKCON Toronto will be able to counteract these allurements with the shimmering images of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the dazzling sounds of *kīrtana,* and the most wonderful packaging of your pearls of wisdom in your books. Your love and personal instructions continue to inspire us to chip away at the edifice of illusion built by the servants of the material energy. We continue to strive toward living your teachings more and more and establishing even more strongly your role as our beloved founder-*ācārya*. We seek your blessings as we continue to try to broaden our outreach, develop a stronger community of devotees, provide support and care for our community, and take care of your beautiful temple at 243 Avenue Road.

We remain eternally indebted to you and humbly fall at your lotus feet. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Your insignificant servants at New Remuṇā Dhāma, ISKCON’s temple community in Toronto, Canada.

## New Tālavana Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. Today I have decided to write your Vyāsa-pūjā offering on behalf of your New Tālavana temple and farm community. We hope you will appreciate it despite its inevitable faults.

Although this offering certainly has its faults, you are faultless. While suffering ordinary or extraordinary circumstances in this world of birth and death, you were fixed in devotional service to your Guru Mahārāja and the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You are always thinking and acting for the benefit of others, and you find infinite pleasure in serving the Supreme Lord. Consequently, there is no room in your divine being for anti–Kṛṣṇa-conscious nonsense.

We are your servants, and we are always ready to carry out your command. It wasn’t easy when we joined you; nor was it easy after you left this world. However, sticking to your lotus feet has continued to provide us shelter. We are very fortunate. We should have drowned in the ocean of material existence, but you taught us the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s *yuga-avatāra* and His *paramparā;* thus you delivered us by your teachings and the wonderful society you created for the purpose of delivering humanity, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

Your ministry will continue to grow and grow because your teachings are the most potent weapons for piercing the darkness of fruitive activity and mental speculation. The devotees in your society will carry on, following in your footsteps; thus they will lead people back home, back to Godhead. Therefore the distribution of your transcendental literatures continues to be a priority in our lives, although we represent another aspect of your mission and much of your unfinished business on this planet.

Your mission’s other aspect and your unfinished business are your farm communities and the establishment of *varṇāśrama*-*dharma*. Your farsighted vision included our society supplying its own food, and members embracing a sense of duty, or dharma. Protecting cows? No other so-called guru in the Western world even considered making this a priority, but you’re different. You’re the pure devotee of the Prince of Vrajabhūmi, and protecting cows is a big priority for Him. Thus you emphasized it. Performing our duties for you meant following in the footsteps of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s dearmost friend, the powerful Pāṇḍava Arjuna. Consequently, thinking and acting according to the teachings of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s *Bhagavad-gītā* is your society’s dharma.

Your books are purifying the hearts of humanity, and the directions you’ve given for living in this world are practical and hardy. Taking care of cows and growing food is real self-sufficiency and rugged individualism. And even more rugged and self-sufficient is the generation of energy from the power of Dharma the bull. You were far ahead of your time, Śrīla Prabhupāda. As a society we are just beginning to catch up to your pure intelligence in regard to farming communities and *varṇāśrama-dharma*.

Will we succeed? If we do, by your mercy, that will be our life’s perfection. But if we fall short, I pray that the future generations of ISKCON devotees will fulfill these ideals for the benefit of humanity, thus adding to the recognition you deserve, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as history’s greatest exponent of God consciousness in the fallen Age of Kali.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are grateful like Bhagavān *Śrī* Kṛṣṇa, and therefore we are hopeful that you will always appreciate our humble selfless service. Therefore let us push on and not waste time constantly looking back. We shall continue to serve the Lord’s *arcā-vigraha* manifestation and distribute His merciful *prasādam* and your transcendental literatures. We will teach others this wonderful science you’ve given us, and simultaneously we will grow food, protect cows, and live according to the dharma of *varṇāśrama,* as espoused by Lord Kṛṣṇa and exhibited by His *yuga*-*avatāra*, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the Golden Avatāra.

This science of the self, its relationship with the Superself, the nature of man, and how man can utilize his nature in God’s service for His pleasure was transmitted to our humble selves by Your Divine Grace, mostly through your *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* and other hallowed writings, as well as through your service, Society, and personal instructions May we never forget any of them and always hear and chant your glories and render service onto your divine lotus feet.

Chanting and dancing in the *harināma*-*saṅkīrtana*-*yajña* and eating the holy remnants of Their Lordships Nitāi-Gaurasundara, we are working our way back to Godhead to join your *saṅkīrtana* party, wherever it may be. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us devotional life and the wonderful opportunity to serve your brilliant perspective and devotees. May we always follow in your footsteps and help others on the path back home, back to Godhead. May we fulfill your great ambition to save the whole world from going to hell. Please bless us to always be one of your trusted servitors.

Your humble servants at New Tālavana Dhāma, in rural southern Mississippi, USA.  
(written by Yogīndra-vandana Dāsa)

## New Varṣāṇā

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on this great occasion!

It gives me great pleasure to let you know that since your first trip down under in 1972, when you traveled all the way from Kṛṣṇa’s transcendental playground, Śrī Vṛndāvana, to this remote land, New Zealand, your ISKCON society here has steadily continued to win favor with the people in general, from the man in the street up to the prime minister.

Very bewildering indeed is Kṛṣṇa’s external energy—He calls it “My divine energy”with really no way out on our own, no matter who we are. The only way out is to surrender to Kṛṣṇa, with deep faith in guru and the previous *ācāryas*—a faith you showed to an amazing degree.

*Śrīla Prabhupāda*, your *Mārkine Bhāgavata-dharma,* the captivating poetic prayer to Kṛṣṇa you wrote on the *Jaladuta* in September 1965, nicely sums up the predicament of us all, and the solution. An excerpt from your English translation of the Bengali original:

Most of the population here is covered by the material modes of ignorance and passion. Absorbed in material life, they think themselves very happy and satisfied, and therefore they have no taste for the transcendental message of Vāsudeva. I do not know how they will be able to understand it. But I know Your causeless mercy can make everything possible because You are the most expert mystic.

By your pure devotion, strong conviction, and hard work, people from all walks of life, East and West, now have the opportunity to learn for the first time the ultimate goal of life: returning home, back to Godhead. This opportunity is very historic. Outside India, devotees are taking to the streets dressed as Vaiṣṇavas, with shaven heads, *dhotīs,* *kurtās,* and saris, and also in Western dress, chanting the holy name and dancing. This has never happened before in the West. It is all by your mercy and your desire to please the Supreme Lord.

There is no doubt that you are bringing great pleasure to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and the Six Gosvāmīs of Vṛndāvana.

You are Kṛṣṇa’s expert mystic. Your movement is coming up on its fiftieth anniversary, and already there are thousands of devotees, millions of books distributed, and millions of people who have tasted *prasādam*.

We are just the first or second generation of pioneers. Kṛṣṇa’s plans are not small. He is the Supreme, and this earth is only one of His planets. Kṛṣṇa has appeared in this age on this planet in His most merciful form, Lord *Gaurāṅga*, and called to the fallen souls of this age: “I have brought the medicine for destroying the illusion of *māyā*. Now pray for this *harināma* *mahā-mantra* and take it.”

How beautiful! It’s free—just take it!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given the world the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, who is freely giving love of Godhead. If not for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, there would be no ISKCON, no *harināma,* no *prasādam,* no Deities, no books, no Māyāpur, no Vṛndāvana, no life.

Thank you for tolerating us.

Your servants at New Varṣāṇā, ISKCON’s farm community in New Zealand.  
(written by Kalasaṁvara Dāsa)

## New Vraja Dhāma

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to the dust of your divine lotus feet!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

All glories to the day when you appeared! All glories to you for coming to the Western world to deliver us from sinful life and give us pure love of Godhead!

Your appearance in this world is a great benediction for us.

While contemplating how you sacrificed everything to carry out your spiritual master’s order and fulfill Lord Caitanya’s prophecy, I remembered this passage in one of your purports:

The prediction of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu will triumph: *pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma/ sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*. “In every town and village, the chanting of My name will be heard.” No one can check the spread of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement because upon this movement is the benediction of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* *Ādi* 17.203, purport\]

It was the Lord’s plan that you come because He wanted to fulfill this prophecy through His great devotee. What a wonderful person you must be, Śrīla Prabhupāda, if the Supreme Lord entrusts you with such a special service!

Lord Caitanya said to Lord *Nityānanda (Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya* 16.64*)*:

*prati-varṣa nīlācale tumi nā āsibā*  
*gauḍe rahi’ mora icchā saphala karibā*

**“Do not come to Jagannātha Purī every year, but stay in Bengal and fulfill My desire.”**

\[PURPORT:\] The mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is to spread the only medicine effective in this fallen Age of Kali—the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. Following the orders of His mother, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu was residing at Jagannātha Purī, and the devotees were coming to see Him. However, the Lord felt that this message must be spread very elaborately in Bengal, and in His absence there was not a second person capable of doing it. Consequently the Lord requested Nityānanda Prabhu to stay there and broadcast the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The Lord also entrusted a similar preaching responsibility to Rūpa Gosvāmī and Sanātana Gosvāmī. Nityānanda Prabhu was requested not to come every year to Jagannātha Purī, although seeing Lord Jagannātha greatly benefits everyone. Does this mean that the Lord was refusing Nityānanda Prabhu a fortunate opportunity? No. One who is a faithful servant of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu must execute His order, even if one has to sacrifice going to Jagannātha Purī to see Lord Jagannātha there. In other words, it is a greater fortune to carry out Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s order than to satisfy one’s senses by seeing Lord Jagannātha.

A question arises upon hearing this pastime: Did the Lord deny you an opportunity to live in Vṛndāvana? Here is your answer to this question:

Preaching Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s cult throughout the world is more important than staying in Vṛndāvana or Jagannātha Purī for one’s own personal satisfaction. Spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness is Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission; therefore His sincere devotees must carry out His desire. \[*Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* *Madhya* 16.64, purport\]

Hearing these words from you means you certainly achieved perfection, because you entirely fulfilled this desire of the Lord’s. You came to the Western world, renouncing the spiritual benefits of the holy *dhāma*, but in fact wherever you are, there is Vṛndāvana:

*bhavad-vidhā bhāgavatās tīrtha-bhūtāḥ svayaṁ vibho*  
*tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni svāntaḥ-sthena gadābhṛtā*

**\[King Yudhiṣṭhira said to Saint Vidura:\] My lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage.**

By manifesting the knowledge in your books, the devotees here in New Vraja-dhāma feel that it has also become Vṛndāvana.

We will never be able to repay our debt to you, *Śrīla Prabhupāda*.

*T*he devotees of New Vraja Dhāma, Krishna Valley, Hungary.  
(written by Saṅkīrtana Dāsa)

## New Vrajamaṇḍala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your divine appearance in this world!

By your unfathomable compassion upon us fallen conditioned souls, you constantly distribute your mercy to your followers by engaging everyone, according to his or her capacity, in the service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Your merciful *vāṇī* association enables many sincere souls to advance quickly on the path of devotional service. Your words enter their hearts, inspiring them to dedicate their lives to the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu—giving the holy name to everyone they meet. People all over the world are being benefited by the association of the members of your transcendental family, who offer the holy name as the only refuge for suffering humanity in Kali-yuga.

Although Śrī Kṛṣṇa owns the “copyright” of His own name, He does not wish to keep this treasure for Himself but desires to share it with others. By His mercy, may all those sincere souls who develop an attraction for His name receive your books and understand the process of *bhakti-yoga*. Then the intelligent and humble followers of your instructions will be able to guide others to the Lord’s lotus feet. These transcendental books are not the fantasy of some fiction writer but have been dictated to you by the Lord personally because you are his pure devotee. Thus He revealed Himself within your heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda. The translations and commentary are therefore perfectly presented by one in full knowledge of the science of Kṛṣṇa.

By reading your transcendental books, anyone—regardless of gender, race, or social, economic, or educational status—can associate with the Lord and His pure devotee and become completely purified through hearing and chanting. As a result, such a fortunate soul will engage all his senses in the loving service of the Lord and His devotees. This is the only process for purifying the mind and senses, making the whole atmosphere auspicious, and coming to the platform of steady devotional service. As Sūta *Gosvāmī* explains to the sages at Naimiṣāraṇya (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 1.2.17–18):

*śṛṇvatāṁ sva-kathāḥ kṛṣṇaḥ puṇya-śravaṇa-kīrtanaḥ*  
*hṛdy antaḥ stho hy abhadrāṇi vidhunoti suhṛt satām*  
*naṣṭa-prāyeṣv abhadreṣu nityaṁ bhāgavata-sevayā*  
*bhagavaty uttama-śloke bhaktir bhavati naiṣṭhikī*

**Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Personality of Godhead, who is the Paramātmā \[Supersoul\] in everyone’s heart and the benefactor of the truthful devotee, cleanses desire for material enjoyment from the heart of the devotee who has developed the urge to hear His messages, which are in themselves virtuous when properly heard and chanted. By regular attendance in classes on the** ***Bhāgavatam*** **and by rendering of service to the pure devotee, all that is troublesome to the heart is almost completely destroyed, and loving service unto the Personality of Godhead, who is praised with transcendental songs, is established as an irrevocable fact.**

We thank you a thousand times for giving us so much, so unconditionally. Begging your mercy to continue in the service of Śrī Śrī Guru and *Gaurāṅga*, we remain

*Your servants* at New Vrajamaṇḍala, ISKCON’s rural community in Spain.  
*(*written by Godrumadvīpa Dāsa)

## New Vrindaban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glory to your lotus feet!

Recently we held a board meeting at which members of various temple departments made presentations on their activities during the past year. One story was particularly memorable:

A highlight for pilgrims during the peak season in New Vrindaban is the Saturday evening swan-boat festival, where Śrī Śrī Rādhā–Vṛndāvana-candra’s *utsava* Deities float around Kusuma Sarovara on a swan-shaped boat while Their devotees and pilgrims chant on the banks and fireworks go off overhead. One Saturday, just as two members of the congregational development team were about to push off and start the festival, they discovered that the boat’s motor was not working. Dismayed at the thought of disappointing the assembled crowd, their fears were confirmed when they quickly realized there was no way of fixing the motor in time, and so they prepared to step off the boat. Suddenly, however, it began to move. At first, they could not identify the cause. After listening for a few moments, though, they noticed that, in place of the steady purring of the motor that they were used to, they heard an intermittent “Splunk!” “Splunk!” “Splunk!” Finally, looking behind the boat, they were astonished to see their department head swimming in the water below. While the two of them had given up hope almost immediately after learning of the motor malfunction, their leader had, without hesitation, pulled off his shirt, jumped into the *kuṇḍa,* and begun kicking, pushing the boat ahead of him. They were so moved by his selfless dedication to serving you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that they began to cry.

New Vrindaban started as a “spiritual frontier,” pioneered by dedicated and dynamic individuals. Their common goal was to fulfill your vision of a self-sufficient farm community and place of pilgrimage in the West. To this end, they devoted countless hours of loving service and incredible amounts of energy and enthusiasm. Some of those pioneers are still with us, as we try to revive this mood of unflagging endeavor to serve your instructions—the same mood illustrated above.

Part of this effort has involved bringing in new blood. For example, we hired a successful local organic farmer to head up our food production. Although not currently a practicing devotee, she has a natural fondness for the community: She visited here with her husband on their wedding anniversary, and when asked to write a geography paper comparing two locales, she chose Vṛndāvana (India) and New Vrindaban. Also, a young man who has grown up in your movement was so inspired by a recent ISKCON leadership seminar that he has taken over management of our guest lodge.

Another part of this effort has involved making the difficult decision to allow natural gas drilling on the property. Although we would never have invited this kind of activity into the *dhāma* on our own initiative, neighboring landowners brought it into our midst. So, making the best use of a bad bargain, we decided to sign leases and employ the funds to pursue your goal of simple living and high thinking; using a thorn to remove a thorn, as Your Divine Grace would sometimes say.

During your last visit here, in June 1976, you explained the role New Vrindaban should play in modern society:

Make this ideal life here. America has got good potency. We have got so much land here. We can have hundreds of New Vrindabans or farms like that. And people will be happy. And invite all the world, ‘Please come and live with us. Why you are suffering congestion, overpopulation? Welcome here. Chant Hare Ka.’ Make that. Indian culture and American strength make the whole world happy.

Please bless us all with the same Brijabasi Spirit that inspired your original New Vrindaban “inmates” so that we can build on the foundation they laid and create the community you wanted—one that is a sanctuary and model for the world.

Your servants at New Vrindaban Dhāma, West Virginia, USA.

## New York City (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa on this earth, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of** ***Bhaktisiddhānta*** **Sarasvatī** ***Gosvāmī*****. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.**

*vande gurūn īśa-bhaktān īśam īśāvatārakān*  
*tat-prakāśāṁś ca tac-chaktīḥ kṛṣṇa-caitanya-saṁjñakam*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto the spiritual masters, the devotees of the Lord, the Lord’s incarnations, His plenary portions, His energies, and the primeval Lord Himself, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya.**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Today we feel most blessed and blissful on this occasion, the wonderful Vyāsa-pūjā celebration of Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I believe Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindadeva especially blessed us by scheduling your appearance day in 1896 on Nandotsava, the day after Śrī Kṛṣṇa Janmāṣṭamī. Kṛṣṇa knew the great spiritual mission Your Divine Grace would establish in this dark and dangerous Kali-yuga, and so, brilliantly shining His wonderful mercy that beautiful September day in 1896 when Your Divine Grace appeared, and which He would again radiantly manifest as the unlimited mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī *Nityānanda* *Rāma*, He blessed all of us, your dedicated followers, with the double ecstasy of a wonderful two-day celebration of bliss, transcendental knowledge, and love, namely Śrī Kṛṣṇa Janmāṣṭamī and Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā. It is a very good day indeed, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for all who serve Your Divine Grace with sincere eagerness, enthusiasm, and unwavering dedication, along with love, devotion, and immovable, unflinching faith. We are always inspired by your kindness, mercy, and love.

On this very special day we may oftentimes glimpse a little taste of divine *bhāva,* perhaps a few tears, perhaps a faltering voice, perhaps a heart and soul mystically affected and blessed by Your Divine Grace and Lord Śrī *Nityānanda* *Rāma*, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, influenced perhaps by the Queen of Vṛndāvana, Śrīmatī *Rādhārāṇī*, and by the transcendental, all-powerful cause of all causes, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, the greatest lover, Bhagavān Śrīla Govindadeva, the master of the *gopīs* and the lover of *Rādhārāṇī*.

*mantra-guru āra yata śikṣā-guru-gaṇa*  
*tāṅhāra caraṇa āge kariye vandana*

**“I first offer my respectful obeisances at the lotus feet of my initiating spiritual master and all my instructing spiritual masters.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.35)**

Our goal, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is unalloyed devotional service offered at your lotus feet, birth after birth. Therefore we aspire to be servants of the servants of the servants of Gaura-Nitāi and Rādhā-Govindadeva. Our aim is to associate in blissful loving relations with the wonderful Vaiṣṇavas who come together to chant the purifying nectar comprising the eternal blissful glories of Kṛṣṇa. We associate safely and blissfully by chanting Kṛṣṇa’s holy names: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

The ever-growing worldwide community of wonderful Vaiṣṇavas has been attracted to this great Hare Kṛṣṇa movement by your unalloyed, pure *bhakti-sevā,* performed in loving obedience to Śrīla *Bhaktisiddhānta* Sarasvatī *Gosvāmī* Mahārāja and offered to the lotus feet of the *Gauḍīya* *sampradāya* predecessor *ācāryas*. This pure *bhakti-sevā,* offered and received as it is, unadulterated, through the chain of Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas,* arriving perfect and complete, delivered at the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, whose desire has thus been fulfilled and who therefore becomes very much pleased upon us all and reciprocates and blesses us all with the soothing rain of His ecstatic bliss and love. Yes, pure *bhakti-sevā,* Śrīla Prabhupāda—the only acceptable prescription for fulfilling the Hare Kṛṣṇa mission, for it alone fulfills the mystical mission of the Golden Avatāra, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, thereby causing Śrī Gaurāṅga’s causeless monsoon of mercy to pour down incessant loving vibrations from His golden heart. *Jaya* Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, Prabhu *Nityānanda*, Jaya Advaita, Gadādhara, Śrīvāsa Ādi-gaura-bhakta-vṛnda! Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare/ Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

We develop love for Kṛṣṇa by Kṛṣṇa conscious activities. Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can know the Supreme Absolute Truth if you bless us with mercy so our blind, sleeping eyes awaken with divine vision. The most confidential secret of success, simple and sublime, is submissive aural reception of the transcendental sounds vibrated by those who know the science of God perfectly, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

The most confidential secret, simple and sublime, is to follow the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness you established for us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are our *śikṣā*-*guru,* Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please show us the simple and sublime path, awaken our eyes with divine vision, reveal to us the Absolute Truth, and remove our fears, our stress, the perpetual anxiety inflicted by the path of darkness, and make our faith stronger than steel, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please guide us to such wonderful transcendent light by shining the transcendental, eternal, and peaceful supreme divine light of *bhagavad*-*bhakti,* of Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu, deep into our dark, sleeping, and lonely hearts, which simply hunger only for pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness, for pure *kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* devotional service, birth after birth, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*śikṣā-guruke ta’ jāni kṛṣṇera svarūpa*  
*antaryāmī, bhakta-śreṣṭha,—ei dui rūpa*

**“One should know the instructing spiritual master to be the Personality of Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa manifests Himself as the Supersoul and as the greatest devotee of the Lord.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta*****,** ***Ādi*** **1.47)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, a devotee must have only one initiating spiritual master, as the scriptures state that having more than one is always forbidden. There is no limit, however, to the number of instructing spiritual masters we may accept. You are the perfect self-realized and Kṛṣṇa-realized *śikṣā*-*guru,* Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your Bhaktivedanta archives of transcendental sound is unsurpassed, delivering the perfection of Kṛṣṇa conscious thought and a treasure house of realization and wisdom. The transcendent wisdom emanates from Bhagavān Śrīla Govindadeva, the supreme author, the original guru, who told you what to write in your wonderful books.

Anyone who is reluctant to accept Your Divine Grace as such a perfect, topmost, self-realized, and Kṛṣṇa-realized *śikṣā-guru* is very unfortunate, for he is sure to be slowed down in his endeavor to go back to Godhead. Indeed, such a person, unfortunately not taking advantage of your empowered *śikṣā*, your transcendental Kṛṣṇa conscious sound vibration, will have to encounter many stumbling blocks on the path of self-realization and Kṛṣṇa-realization. Such a person will thereby lose the great opportunity at hand: to achieve by your mercy the perfection of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the topmost goal of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti-yoga*, in this precious and short lifetime, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Please bless us today, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please force us by the power of your mercy to avoid becoming aimlessly floating souls on the ocean of nescience, like ships without rudders. Śrīla Prabhupāda, by accepting your wise guidance as our preeminent *śikṣā-guru,* we are in fact accepting a miraculous blessing in our conditioned and sorrowful hearts. A miraculous blessing needed by so many unfortunate hearts, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

May your unsurpassed *śikṣā* kindle our hearts with peace, ignite them with happiness, and awaken them with love, beginning with those inherent eternal blissful emotions of *bhava-bhakti,* leading to the ultimate transcendental vibration of *kṛṣṇa-prema*. Some day, by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda—yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda. When, oh when, will that day be ours?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is therefore of the utmost urgency that every sincere disciple and every person aspiring to be a sincere disciple accept bona fide spiritual masters in terms of the śāstric injunctions. Both a *dīkṣā*-*guru* and *śikṣā*-*gurus*. Śrīla Jīva *Gosvāmī*, a great *ācārya* and wonderful *śikṣā*-*guru,* advises us not to accept a spiritual master in terms of hereditary or customary social and ecclesiastical conventions. One should simply try to find a genuinely qualified, bona fide, self-realized spiritual master for real and substantial advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ’ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caitya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.29.6)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, while others give us help in showing the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the spiritual master who initiates us with the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra* is to be known as the initiator, the *dīkṣā*-*guru,* and the saintly persons who provide instructions for our progressive advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness are called instructing spiritual masters, *śikṣā-gurus*. The initiating and instructing spiritual masters are equal and identical manifestations of Kṛṣṇa, although they have different dealings.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrī *Nityānanda* *Rāma* have mercifully blessed us by establishing this divine system of transcendent dissemination of *divya*-*jñāna,* the blessing of transcendental knowledge and self-realization and Kṛṣṇa-realization. By the mercy of Gaura-Nitāi, all those who follow your perfect Kṛṣṇa conscious *śikṣā* shall achieve the topmost goal of perfect and complete Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and at the end all such perfect *śikṣā* followers shall be qualified to return home, back to Godhead.
